### packets/FP-MCP-297.md
11:After FP-MCP-296 approval validation, the next intended phase was approved pre-start evidence followed by state snapshot evidence. Instead, the state-snapshot dry-run tool was invoked before approved pre-start evidence existed. Each attempt failed closed with PRE_START_EVIDENCE_INVALID, PRE_START_EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE, ARTIFACT_HASH_MISSING, STATE_SNAPSHOT_NOT_RECORDED, and execution-disabled reasons. The failures were non-executing: startEndpointContacted false, opencodeStarted false, runnerContactedForStart false, preStartSnapshotRecorded false, postStartSnapshotRecorded false. Because the approval was short-lived and the sequence had already deviated, the correct action is to hold, allow the approval to expire if necessary, and resume later with a fresh approval and the correct order: record approved pre-start evidence first, validate it, then record/validate state snapshots immediately before any future start-boundary work.

### packets/FP-MCP-304.md
11:FP-MCP-303 proved the FP-027 path now reaches a valid local clearance but remains correctly blocked by GLOBAL_DISABLE_ACTIVE, RUNNER_EXECUTION_DISABLED, and OPENCODE_EXECUTION_DISABLED. FP-MCP-304 records the design contract for the missing bridge between eligible local clearance and guarded runner start. The enablement gate should be a scoped, local, short-lived, single-use artifact bound to packet, requestId, approvalId, clearanceId, modelId, runMode, workspace, branch, execution base commit, current request-artifact/evidence HEAD, pre-start evidence hash, state snapshot attempt/hash pair, and runner capability observation. It must never clear execution broadly, never create a standing global unlock, never authorize direct HTTP execution, and never start OpenCode or contact the runner start endpoint when it is created. A future guarded start may proceed only when fresh orientation reports executionAllowedNow true, preflightPassableNow true, disableSwitch passed, opencodeReadiness passed, and all prior evidence gates remain valid.

### packets/FP-MCP-340.md
11:Record the read-only implementation finding that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate accepts applyPreviewFollowOn?: boolean. At PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, the wrapper returns preview-only when applyPreviewFollowOn is absent or false. It invokes createOperatorLocalExecutionClearance only after a successful preview and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. The repeated-preview result in FP-MCP-339 was therefore caused by a missing follow-on confirmation argument, not by host blocking, approval failure, or raw-tool exposure.

## FP-027 / canary / runner-start search across runs 297-368

### runs/FP-MCP-297
runs/FP-MCP-297/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-297 records that the FP-MCP-295 minimal canary did not advance past approval into pre-start evidence or state snapshots: repeated non-executing state-snapshot dry-run attempts failed closed because approved pre-start evidence was missing, so the operator stopped rather than rushing toward the approval expiration window; no runner start endpoint was contacted, no OpenCode worker/session was started, no approval was consumed, no disable switch was cleared, no snapshots were recorded, and FP-027 did not run.
runs/FP-MCP-297/result-summary.md:11:After FP-MCP-296 approval validation, the next intended phase was approved pre-start evidence followed by state snapshot evidence. Instead, the state-snapshot dry-run tool was invoked before approved pre-start evidence existed. Each attempt failed closed with PRE_START_EVIDENCE_INVALID, PRE_START_EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE, ARTIFACT_HASH_MISSING, STATE_SNAPSHOT_NOT_RECORDED, and execution-disabled reasons. The failures were non-executing: startEndpointContacted false, opencodeStarted false, runnerContactedForStart false, preStartSnapshotRecorded false, postStartSnapshotRecorded false. Because the approval was short-lived and the sequence had already deviated, the correct action is to hold, allow the approval to expire if necessary, and resume later with a fresh approval and the correct order: record approved pre-start evidence first, validate it, then record/validate state snapshots immediately before any future start-boundary work.

runs/FP-MCP-297/v1o-evidence-recording-result.json:10:  "admitted_claim": "FP-MCP-297 records that the FP-MCP-295 minimal canary did not advance past approval into pre-start evidence or state snapshots: repeated non-executing state-snapshot dry-run attempts failed closed because approved pre-start evidence was missing, so the operator stopped rather than rushing toward the approval expiration window; no runner start endpoint was contacted, no OpenCode worker/session was started, no approval was consumed, no disable switch was cleared, no snapshots were recorded, and FP-027 did not run.",

runs/FP-MCP-297/fail-closed-hold-record.json:6:  "summary": "After FP-MCP-296 approval validation, the next intended phase was approved pre-start evidence followed by state snapshot evidence. ### runs/FP-MCP-298
runs/FP-MCP-298/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-298 turns the FP-027 canary order from operator memory into an explicit mechanical sequencing guard: state snapshot recording is forbidden until approved pre-start evidence exists and validates; the next legal action is fresh approval if the prior approval expired, otherwise approved pre-start evidence recording, followed by pre-start validation before any state snapshot attempt.
runs/FP-MCP-298/result-summary.md:11:FP-MCP-298 records a non-executing sequence guard after FP-MCP-297 exposed an operator-side out-of-order attempt. The guard defines ordered FP-027 canary gates and a current next-action rule so later steps cannot be treated as interchangeable. It preserves the fact that request artifact FP-MCP-295 / REQ-20260704T142232816Z-a82a42b6 exists and was validated, and approval FP-MCP-296 / APPROVAL-20260704T142523179Z-10b0b6f3 was validated but expires at 2026-07-04T14:40:00.000Z. If continuation occurs after expiration, fresh approval is the only next legal action. If a valid unexpired approval exists, the next legal action is to record approved pre-start evidence, then validate it. State snapshot recording remains forbidden until approved pre-start evidence has both been recorded and validated. This checkpoint does not implement new bridge code, create approval, create pre-start evidence, record snapshots, clear disable switches, create clearance, contact runner start, start OpenCode, consume approval, or run FP-027.

### runs/FP-MCP-303
runs/FP-MCP-303/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-303 records that the FP-MCP-295 minimal FP-027 guarded/worker canary advanced through fresh approval, refreshed clearance preview, local clearance creation, and independent local clearance validation, but did not proceed to runner start because the aggregate execution orientation still reported executionAllowedNow false, globalDisableActive true, runnerExecutionEnabled false, opencodeExecutionEnabled false, and guarded preflight failed the disableSwitch and opencodeReadiness gates; no exposed scoped disable-clear or execution-enable tool was available, so no runner start endpoint was contacted and OpenCode was not started.
runs/FP-MCP-303/result-summary.md:11:The operator requested proceeding until actually running ForgePilot OpenCode. A fresh approval was recorded as FP-MCP-302 approval APPROVAL-20260704T145343543Z-8a4d2a6f, committed at 9a0b6b7, and validated. A refreshed clearance preview at current HEAD 9a0b6b7 passed and local clearance CLEARANCE-20260704T145411583Z-29b22ae0 was created by mutating only the gitignored MCP bridge local clearance file. Independent clearance validation reported eligibleForGuardedAttempt true. However, the read-only execution gate orientation still reported the execution enablement blockers: GLOBAL_DISABLE_ACTIVE, RUNNER_EXECUTION_DISABLED, and OPENCODE_EXECUTION_DISABLED. Guarded preflight showed request artifact, commit binding, model/run mode, runner capability, pre-start evidence, state snapshot evidence, human approval evidence, and evidence ledger readiness passed, but disableSwitch failed and opencodeReadiness failed. Because there is no available scoped tool to clear the disable switch or enable runner/OpenCode execution, and because runner start is forbidden before those gates pass, the correct endpoint is a blocked-at-enablement checkpoint rather than contacting runner start.
runs/FP-MCP-303/result-summary.md:15:No execution was authorized. No approval was consumed. The disable switch was not cleared. The runner start endpoint was not contacted. OpenCode was not started. FP-027 did not run.

### runs/FP-MCP-304
runs/FP-MCP-304/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-304 defines the missing scoped execution enablement gate needed after local clearance and before any guarded runner start: execution enablement must be represented as a short-lived, single-use, exact-tuple local artifact that can make executionAllowedNow true only for one approved FP-MCP-295 guarded-run attempt, without globally disabling safety, without standing authorization, without direct HTTP execution authority, and without contacting runner start by itself.
runs/FP-MCP-304/result-summary.md:11:FP-MCP-303 proved the FP-027 path now reaches a valid local clearance but remains correctly blocked by GLOBAL_DISABLE_ACTIVE, RUNNER_EXECUTION_DISABLED, and OPENCODE_EXECUTION_DISABLED. FP-MCP-304 records the design contract for the missing bridge between eligible local clearance and guarded runner start. The enablement gate should be a scoped, local, short-lived, single-use artifact bound to packet, requestId, approvalId, clearanceId, modelId, runMode, workspace, branch, execution base commit, current request-artifact/evidence HEAD, pre-start evidence hash, state snapshot attempt/hash pair, and runner capability observation. It must never clear execution broadly, never create a standing global unlock, never authorize direct HTTP execution, and never start OpenCode or contact the runner start endpoint when it is created. A future guarded start may proceed only when fresh orientation reports executionAllowedNow true, preflightPassableNow true, disableSwitch passed, opencodeReadiness passed, and all prior evidence gates remain valid.

runs/FP-MCP-316/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-316 was implemented in the MCP bridge by extending forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate to support the sequencer-selected scoped enablement preview/create gate only, while preserving the stop-before-runner-start boundary.
runs/FP-MCP-316/result-summary.md:11:The MCP bridge implementation on branch feature/oauth-auth0 added one executor branch for forgepilot_preview_operator_local_scoped_execution_enablement_review_manifest. The branch builds typed scoped enablement preview/create arguments from sequencer output, previews the scoped enablement review manifest, optionally applies the follow-on scoped enablement creation when applyPreviewFollowOn is true and preview has no reasons, and reports PREVIEW_AND_CREATE_SCOPED_ENABLEMENT boundary fields. No runner-start path, approval consumption, OpenCode start, or canary execution path was added.
runs/FP-MCP-316/result-summary.md:15:No execution was authorized. No approval was consumed. The disable switch was not cleared. The runner start endpoint was not contacted. OpenCode was not started. FP-027 did not run.

Number(args.expiresInMinutes ?? 5),\\n    approval: \"PREVIEW_OPERATOR_LOCAL_SCOPED_EXECUTION_ENABLEMENT_REVIEW_MANIFEST\" as const\\n  };\\n}\\nfunction typedScopedEnablementCreateArgs(args: Record<string, unknown>, minutes: number | undefined) {\\n  const previewArgs = typedScopedEnablementArgs(args, minutes);\\n  return { ...previewArgs, approval: \"CREATE_OPERATOR_LOCAL_SCOPED_EXECUTION_ENABLEMENT\" as const };\\n}\\n'''\nif new_funcs in s:\n    raise SystemExit('enablement typed args already present')\nif insert_after not in s:\n    raise nextToolName,\\n    decision,\\n    result: null,\\n    reasons: [\"NEXT_GATE_NOT_SUPPORTED_BY_EXECUTOR_V1\"],\\n    boundary: boundary(\"UNSUPPORTED_GATE\")\\n  };\\n}\\n'''\nbranch='''\\n  if typedScopedEnablementCreateArgs(args, input.enablementExpiresInMinutes);\\n    const created = await createOperatorLocalScopedExecutionEnablement(createArgs);\\n    const createdBoundary = asRecord(asRecord(created).boundary);\\n    return {\\n      checkedAt,\\n      applied: true,\\n      nextRequiredGate,\\n      nextToolName,\\n      decision,\\n      result: { preview, followOnTool: \"forgepilot_create_operator_local_scoped_execution_enablement\", created },\\n      followOnApplied: true,\\n      reasons: reasonsOf(created),\\n      boundary: boundary(\"PREVIEW_AND_CREATE_SCOPED_ENABLEMENT\", {\\n        filesWritten: bool(createdBoundary.filesWritten),\\n        enablementCreated: bool(createdBoundary.enablementCreated),\\n        enablementMutated: bool(createdBoundary.enablementMutated)\\n      })\\n    };\\n  }\\n'''\nif branch in s:\n    raise SystemExit('enablement executor branch already present')\nif branch_anchor not in ### runs/FP-MCP-319
runs/FP-MCP-319/result-summary.md:6:FP-MCP-319 successfully prepared the FP-MCP-295 canary tuple through fresh approval, fresh operator-local clearance, fresh runner capability observation, and fresh scoped execution enablement; the read-only sequencer confirmed the next gate was the final runner-start follow-on, but the attempted guarded runner-start executor call was blocked by the platform safety layer before reaching the MCP tool.
runs/FP-MCP-319/result-summary.md:11:A fresh FP-MCP-319 approval was recorded, committed at c38ce9b, and validated as usable. The guarded executor created/replaced clearance CLEARANCE-20260704T185325927Z-83b53937 and scoped enablement ENABLEMENT-20260704T185336801Z-2701a18c, both bound to the FP-MCP-295 tuple and current core commit c38ce9b. A fresh runner capability observation at 2026-07-04T18:53:29.009Z confirmed the runner start capability was callable guarded. The final guarded executor call intended to route one bounded runner-start attempt through forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate was blocked by OpenAI platform safety checks before MCP execution. A subsequent read-only sequencer check confirmed request, approval, clearance, enablement, and evidence were valid and the next gate was FRESH_ORIENTATION_BEFORE_START with followOnAfterSuccess forgepilot_start_remote_runner_request.
runs/FP-MCP-319/result-summary.md:15:No execution was authorized. No approval was consumed. The disable switch was not cleared. The runner start endpoint was not contacted. OpenCode was not started. FP-027 did not run.

### runs/FP-MCP-322
runs/FP-MCP-322/result-summary.md:11:The MCP bridge now contains scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs. The script uses the installed MCP SDK streamable HTTP client, requires the bearer token from the operator environment, allows only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate, accepts input and output file paths, and writes a structured result JSON. The script was syntax-checked with node --check and committed in the bridge as 11d4490.
runs/FP-MCP-322/result-summary.md:15:No execution was authorized. No approval was consumed. The disable switch was not cleared. The runner start endpoint was not contacted. OpenCode was not started. FP-027 did not run.
runs/FP-MCP-322/terminal-commands/TERM-20260704T190818912Z-55b979d2-request.json:7:  "command": "cat > scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';\nimport { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';\nimport { CallToolResultSchema } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';\n\nfunction usage() {\n  console.error('Usage: node scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs --input <input.json> --output <result.json> [--url http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp]');\n  console.error('Requires FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN in the environment for bearer auth.');\n}\n\nfunction argValue(name) {\n  const index = process.argv.indexOf(name);\n  if (index === -1) return null;\n  return process.argv[index + 1] ?? null;\n}\n\nconst inputPath = argValue('--input');\nconst outputPath = argValue('--output');\nconst url = argValue('--url') ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp';\nconst token = process.env.FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN;\n\nif (!inputPath || !outputPath) {\n  usage();\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nif (!token) {\n  console.error('FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN is required but was not set.');\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nconst input = JSON.parse(await readFile(inputPath, 'utf8'));\nif (input.tool !== 'forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate') {\n  throw new Error('Only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate is allowed by this local handoff script.');\n}\nif (!input.arguments || typeof input.arguments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input.arguments)) {\n  throw new Error('Input must contain an object field named arguments.');\n}\n\nconst client = new Client({ name: 'forgepilot-local-guarded-apply', version: '1.0.0' }, { capabilities: {} });\nconst transport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(url), {\n  requestInit: {\n    headers: {\n      authorization: `Bearer ${token}`\n    }\n  }\n});\n\ntry {\n  await client.connect(transport);\n  const result = await client.request({\n    method: 'tools/call',\n    params: {\n      name: input.tool,\n      arguments: input.arguments\n    }\n  }, CallToolResultSchema);\n\n  const output = {\n    schemaVersion: 'FP-MCP-322-local-guarded-apply-result-v1',\n    recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    mcpUrl: url,\n    tool: input.tool,\n    arguments: input.arguments,\n    result\n  };\n\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });\n  await writeFile(outputPath, JSON.stringify(output, null, 2) + '\\n', 'utf8');\n  console.log(`Wrote ${outputPath}`);\n} finally {\n  await client.close();\n}\nEOF\nchmod +x scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs\nnode --check scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs",
runs/FP-MCP-322/terminal-commands/TERM-20260704T190818912Z-55b979d2-result.json:8:  "command": "cat > scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';\nimport { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';\nimport { CallToolResultSchema } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';\n\nfunction usage() {\n  console.error('Usage: node scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs --input <input.json> --output <result.json> [--url http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp]');\n  console.error('Requires FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN in the environment for bearer auth.');\n}\n\nfunction argValue(name) {\n  const index = process.argv.indexOf(name);\n  if (index === -1) return null;\n  return process.argv[index + 1] ?? null;\n}\n\nconst inputPath = argValue('--input');\nconst outputPath = argValue('--output');\nconst url = argValue('--url') ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp';\nconst token = process.env.FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN;\n\nif (!inputPath || !outputPath) {\n  usage();\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nif (!token) {\n  console.error('FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN is required but was not set.');\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nconst input = JSON.parse(await readFile(inputPath, 'utf8'));\nif (input.tool !== 'forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate') {\n  throw new Error('Only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate is allowed by this local handoff script.');\n}\nif (!input.arguments || typeof input.arguments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input.arguments)) {\n  throw new Error('Input must contain an object field named arguments.');\n}\n\nconst client = new Client({ name: 'forgepilot-local-guarded-apply', version: '1.0.0' }, { capabilities: {} });\nconst transport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(url), {\n  requestInit: {\n    headers: {\n      authorization: `Bearer ${token}`\n    }\n  }\n});\n\ntry {\n  await client.connect(transport);\n  const result = await client.request({\n    method: 'tools/call',\n    params: {\n      name: input.tool,\n      arguments: input.arguments\n    }\n  }, CallToolResultSchema);\n\n  const output = {\n    schemaVersion: 'FP-MCP-322-local-guarded-apply-result-v1',\n    recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    mcpUrl: url,\n    tool: input.tool,\n    arguments: input.arguments,\n    result\n  };\n\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });\n  await writeFile(outputPath, JSON.stringify(output, null, 2) + '\\n', 'utf8');\n  console.log(`Wrote ${outputPath}`);\n} finally {\n  await client.close();\n}\nEOF\nchmod +x scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs\nnode --check scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs",

### runs/FP-MCP-339
runs/FP-MCP-339/result-summary.md:11:Record that FP-MCP-338 created and validated a fresh approval, then called forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate twice. Both wrapper calls were allowed by the host. The first call returned preview-only PREVIEW_CLEARANCE as expected. The second call also returned PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, wrote no files, consumed no approval, did not clear controls, did not contact the runner start endpoint, and did not start OpenCode. This suggests the wrapper does not currently advance from preview to clearance creation without an additional confirmation signal or implementation change.

### runs/FP-MCP-340
runs/FP-MCP-340/result-summary.md:11:Record the read-only implementation finding that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate accepts applyPreviewFollowOn?: boolean. At PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, the wrapper returns preview-only when applyPreviewFollowOn is absent or false. It invokes createOperatorLocalExecutionClearance only after a successful preview and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. The repeated-preview result in FP-MCP-339 was therefore caused by a missing follow-on confirmation argument, not by host blocking, approval failure, or raw-tool exposure.
runs/FP-MCP-340/result-summary.md:15:No execution was authorized. No approval was consumed. The disable switch was not cleared. The runner start endpoint was not contacted. OpenCode was not started. FP-027 did not run.

packets/FP-MCP-344.md:11:Record that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate was allowed by the ChatGPT host when called with debug packet/request/approval context and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. ForgePilot safely rejected the call because the request artifact was missing or invalid and the next gate was unsupported by the executor. No files were written, no approval was consumed, no runner endpoint was contacted, and OpenCode was not started.
packets/FP-MCP-322.md:11:The MCP bridge now contains scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs. The script uses the installed MCP SDK streamable HTTP client, requires the bearer token from the operator environment, allows only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate, accepts input and output file paths, and writes a structured result JSON. The script was syntax-checked with node --check and committed in the bridge as 11d4490.
packets/FP-MCP-310.md:11:FP-MCP-310 approval APPROVAL-20260704T160142146Z-6a78dc28 was recorded for FP-MCP-295 request REQ-20260704T142232816Z-a82a42b6, committed at 46b1e59, and validated usable. After validation, the intended next action was to ask forgepilot_get_guarded_canary_next_gate. Instead, the adjacent read-only guarded preflight report tool was selected repeatedly. The operator recorded trace TRACE-20260704T160244953Z-842f0b92 at commit 9d6d412 and stopped the path.
packets/FP-MCP-308.md:11:Implemented src/guarded-canary-gate-sequencer.ts and registered forgepilot_get_guarded_canary_next_gate in src/server.ts. The tool inspects current state and returns nextRequiredGate, nextToolName, nextToolArguments, followOnAfterSuccess, reasons, and boundary. MCP bridge pnpm build passed and pnpm test passed.
packets/FP-MCP-336.md:11:Record that FP-MCP-335 validated approval allowed forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate to apply the PREVIEW_CLEARANCE wrapper phase. The result was preview-only, wrote no files, consumed no approval, did not clear controls, did not contact the runner start endpoint, and did not start OpenCode.
packets/FP-MCP-334.md:11:Record that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate was allowed by the ChatGPT host at the PREVIEW_CLEARANCE stage. ForgePilot did not apply the gate because the FP-MCP-331 approval was expired or invalid. No files were written, no approval was consumed, no runner endpoint was contacted, and OpenCode was not started.

packets/FP-MCP-362.md:1:# FP-MCP-362 — Guarded sequencer refactor and MCP conformance plan
packets/FP-MCP-362.md:9:This packet records the governing plan for guarded sequencer work after completion of the inline MCP registration extraction series.
packets/FP-MCP-362.md:11:The plan requires evidence-first diagnosis of the false-positive guarded sequencer next-gate behavior before implementing any correction. It also defines MCP and OpenAI conformance standards that future sequencer packets must preserve.
packets/FP-MCP-362.md:15:    FP-MCP-362 records the required work order, anti-false-positive invariant, and MCP/OpenAI conformance standards for future guarded sequencer work.

packets/FP-MCP-349.md:11:A fresh structured approval was recorded under FP-MCP-346, committed at 46642ae, and then supplied to forgepilot_observe_next_gate. The observer reported approval valid and advanced nextRequiredGate to PREVIEW_CLEARANCE. The follow-on read-only forgepilot_preview_clearance_creation call returned previewOnly=true and preserved all non-execution boundaries, but preflight.eligibleToInvokeClearancePopup was false because currentCommitMatches failed: expected head 5c0ab8b while current head was 46642ae. No clearance intent was recorded.
packets/FP-MCP-349.md:28:- Did not call deprecated forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate.
packets/FP-MCP-339.md:11:Record that FP-MCP-338 created and validated a fresh approval, then called forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate twice. Both wrapper calls were allowed by the host. The first call returned preview-only PREVIEW_CLEARANCE as expected. The second call also returned PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, wrote no files, consumed no approval, did not clear controls, did not contact the runner start endpoint, and did not start OpenCode. This suggests the wrapper does not currently advance from preview to clearance creation without an additional confirmation signal or implementation change.
packets/FP-MCP-330.md:11:Record the observed distinction between a hot session that blocked execution-adjacent MCP calls and a fresh session that allowed read-only status, OpenCode orientation, disable-switch checks, execution enablement checks, checkpoint recording, and the read-only guarded sequencer with the real FP-MCP-295 request tuple.
packets/FP-MCP-330.md:16:Fresh-session characterization indicates the prior ChatGPT MCP block was not caused by the real request tuple, checkpoint recording, OpenCode read-only probes, or the read-only guarded sequencer itself.
packets/FP-MCP-330.md:50:Fresh-session characterization indicates the prior ChatGPT MCP block was not caused by the real request tuple, checkpoint recording, OpenCode read-only probes, or the read-only guarded sequencer itself.

The enforced executor tool forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate remains registered outside the narrow-surface guard. TypeScript compilation passed after the change.

The approval artifact was tracked and committed, but the observer reported valid=false with reason APPROVAL_EXPIRED_OR_INVALID because expiresAt was 2026-07-05T09:50:00.000Z and checkedAt was 2026-07-05T09:59:30.068Z. The capability-separated observer remained read-only and reported no files written, no approval consumed, no runner start endpoint contact, no OpenCode start, and no guarded canary run.

packets/FP-MCP-364A.md:1:# FP-MCP-364A — Runtime validation after guarded sequencer correction
packets/FP-MCP-364A.md:11:FP-MCP-364 corrected the guarded sequencer false-positive path diagnosed in FP-MCP-363. The correction propagates the observed current clearance id from the validated clearance artifact into enablement validation so stale enablement evidence cannot satisfy the enablement prerequisite when the caller omits input.clearanceId.
packets/FP-MCP-364A.md:25:      bridgeChange: Fix guarded sequencer stale enablement clearance validation
packets/FP-MCP-364A.md:30:      correctedPath: getGuardedCanaryNextGate -> clearanceStatus -> observedClearanceId -> enablementStatus
packets/FP-MCP-312.md:11:The MCP bridge repository feature/oauth-auth0 advanced from 6b6fcc3 to 5a75db7 with commit 'Add guarded canary next-gate executor'. The implementation adds src/guarded-canary-gate-executor.ts and registers forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate in server.ts. V1 asks the sequencer internally, blocks if the sequencer requires fresh human approval, supports the sequencer-selected clearance preview gate, and when applyPreviewFollowOn is true creates clearance internally only through forgepilot_create_operator_local_execution_clearance. TypeScript compilation passed. Direct packet-id lint/test commands were blocked by the platform filter during this run, but the new executor file was checked for hardcoded packet IDs and contained none.
packets/FP-MCP-366A.md:11:Bridge commit 32f49e6 was built and the forgepilot-chatgpt-mcp user service was restarted. The live Actions manifest exposed forgepilot_observe_next_gate as a public ChatGPT-safe tool with the sanitized observer description.
packets/FP-MCP-366A.md:17:    FP-MCP-366A confirms that the sanitized read-only guarded sequencer observer is visible in the live ChatGPT-safe MCP Actions surface while preserving the non-execution runtime boundary.
packets/FP-MCP-366A.md:25:      bridgeCommitMessage: Expose sanitized guarded sequencer observer to safe surface
packets/FP-MCP-366A.md:40:      observerToolName: forgepilot_observe_next_gate
packets/FP-MCP-340.md:11:Record the read-only implementation finding that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate accepts applyPreviewFollowOn?: boolean. At PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, the wrapper returns preview-only when applyPreviewFollowOn is absent or false. It invokes createOperatorLocalExecutionClearance only after a successful preview and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. The repeated-preview result in FP-MCP-339 was therefore caused by a missing follow-on confirmation argument, not by host blocking, approval failure, or raw-tool exposure.

runs/FP-MCP-322/result-summary.md:11:The MCP bridge now contains scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs. The script uses the installed MCP SDK streamable HTTP client, requires the bearer token from the operator environment, allows only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate, accepts input and output file paths, and writes a structured result JSON. The script was syntax-checked with node --check and committed in the bridge as 11d4490.
runs/FP-MCP-322/terminal-commands/TERM-20260704T190818912Z-55b979d2-request.json:7:  "command": "cat > scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';\nimport { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';\nimport { CallToolResultSchema } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';\n\nfunction usage() {\n  console.error('Usage: node scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs --input <input.json> --output <result.json> [--url http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp]');\n  console.error('Requires FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN in the environment for bearer auth.');\n}\n\nfunction argValue(name) {\n  const index = process.argv.indexOf(name);\n  if (index === -1) return null;\n  return process.argv[index + 1] ?? null;\n}\n\nconst inputPath = argValue('--input');\nconst outputPath = argValue('--output');\nconst url = argValue('--url') ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp';\nconst token = process.env.FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN;\n\nif (!inputPath || !outputPath) {\n  usage();\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nif (!token) {\n  console.error('FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN is required but was not set.');\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nconst input = JSON.parse(await readFile(inputPath, 'utf8'));\nif (input.tool !== 'forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate') {\n  throw new Error('Only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate is allowed by this local handoff script.');\n}\nif (!input.arguments || typeof input.arguments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input.arguments)) {\n  throw new Error('Input must contain an object field named arguments.');\n}\n\nconst client = new Client({ name: 'forgepilot-local-guarded-apply', version: '1.0.0' }, { capabilities: {} });\nconst transport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(url), {\n  requestInit: {\n    headers: {\n      authorization: `Bearer ${token}`\n    }\n  }\n});\n\ntry {\n  await client.connect(transport);\n  const result = await client.request({\n    method: 'tools/call',\n    params: {\n      name: input.tool,\n      arguments: input.arguments\n    }\n  }, CallToolResultSchema);\n\n  const output = {\n    schemaVersion: 'FP-MCP-322-local-guarded-apply-result-v1',\n    recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    mcpUrl: url,\n    tool: input.tool,\n    arguments: input.arguments,\n    result\n  };\n\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });\n  await writeFile(outputPath, JSON.stringify(output, null, 2) + '\\n', 'utf8');\n  console.log(`Wrote ${outputPath}`);\n} finally {\n  await client.close();\n}\nEOF\nchmod +x scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs\nnode --check scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs",
runs/FP-MCP-322/terminal-commands/TERM-20260704T190818912Z-55b979d2-result.json:8:  "command": "cat > scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs <<'EOF'\n#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { Client } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/index.js';\nimport { StreamableHTTPClientTransport } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/client/streamableHttp.js';\nimport { CallToolResultSchema } from '@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js';\n\nfunction usage() {\n  console.error('Usage: node scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs --input <input.json> --output <result.json> [--url http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp]');\n  console.error('Requires FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN in the environment for bearer auth.');\n}\n\nfunction argValue(name) {\n  const index = process.argv.indexOf(name);\n  if (index === -1) return null;\n  return process.argv[index + 1] ?? null;\n}\n\nconst inputPath = argValue('--input');\nconst outputPath = argValue('--output');\nconst url = argValue('--url') ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8787/mcp';\nconst token = process.env.FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN;\n\nif (!inputPath || !outputPath) {\n  usage();\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nif (!token) {\n  console.error('FORGEPILOT_MCP_TOKEN is required but was not set.');\n  process.exit(2);\n}\n\nconst input = JSON.parse(await readFile(inputPath, 'utf8'));\nif (input.tool !== 'forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate') {\n  throw new Error('Only forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate is allowed by this local handoff script.');\n}\nif (!input.arguments || typeof input.arguments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(input.arguments)) {\n  throw new Error('Input must contain an object field named arguments.');\n}\n\nconst client = new Client({ name: 'forgepilot-local-guarded-apply', version: '1.0.0' }, { capabilities: {} });\nconst transport = new StreamableHTTPClientTransport(new URL(url), {\n  requestInit: {\n    headers: {\n      authorization: `Bearer ${token}`\n    }\n  }\n});\n\ntry {\n  await client.connect(transport);\n  const result = await client.request({\n    method: 'tools/call',\n    params: {\n      name: input.tool,\n      arguments: input.arguments\n    }\n  }, CallToolResultSchema);\n\n  const output = {\n    schemaVersion: 'FP-MCP-322-local-guarded-apply-result-v1',\n    recordedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    mcpUrl: url,\n    tool: input.tool,\n    arguments: input.arguments,\n    result\n  };\n\n  await mkdir(path.dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });\n  await writeFile(outputPath, JSON.stringify(output, null, 2) + '\\n', 'utf8');\n  console.log(`Wrote ${outputPath}`);\n} finally {\n  await client.close();\n}\nEOF\nchmod +x scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs\nnode --check scripts/local-guarded-apply.mjs",

runs/FP-MCP-340/result-summary.md:11:Record the read-only implementation finding that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate accepts applyPreviewFollowOn?: boolean. At PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, the wrapper returns preview-only when applyPreviewFollowOn is absent or false. It invokes createOperatorLocalExecutionClearance only after a successful preview and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. The repeated-preview result in FP-MCP-339 was therefore caused by a missing follow-on confirmation argument, not by host blocking, approval failure, or raw-tool exposure.
runs/FP-MCP-340/contract-clarification-record.json:6:  "summary": "Record the read-only implementation finding that forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate accepts applyPreviewFollowOn?: boolean. At PREVIEW_CLEARANCE, the wrapper returns preview-only when applyPreviewFollowOn is absent or false. It invokes createOperatorLocalExecutionClearance only after a successful preview and applyPreviewFollowOn: true. The repeated-preview result in FP-MCP-339 was therefore caused by a missing follow-on confirmation argument, not by host blocking, approval failure, or raw-tool exposure.",

runs/FP-MCP-312/result-summary.md:11:The MCP bridge repository feature/oauth-auth0 advanced from 6b6fcc3 to 5a75db7 with commit 'Add guarded canary next-gate executor'. The implementation adds src/guarded-canary-gate-executor.ts and registers forgepilot_apply_guarded_canary_next_gate in server.ts. V1 asks the sequencer internally, blocks if the sequencer requires fresh human approval, supports the sequencer-selected clearance preview gate, and when applyPreviewFollowOn is true creates clearance internally only through forgepilot_create_operator_local_execution_clearance. TypeScript compilation passed. Direct packet-id lint/test commands were blocked by the platform filter during this run, but the new executor file was checked for hardcoded packet IDs and contained none.

packets/FP-MCP-289.md:11:FP-MCP-289 records the auditor-requested reconciliation before any MCP server refactor proceeds. It accounts for admitted evidence IDs 153 through 163, clarifies that FP-MCP-278 became the observed MCP refactor workload decision while FP-MCP-279 performed the master-plan orientation refresh, clarifies the FP-MCP-277 COMPENSATED checklist item, and corrects the direct-HTTP pivot language. The remote-runner guarded-start path retains safety properties that the direct HTTP path has not yet proven: process/state snapshots at contact boundary, live abort-readiness equivalent to process-group abort, no-retry/no-chaining state-machine evidence, approval-consumption coupling, and FP-027 canary discipline. The direct HTTP path has proven authenticated reachability, session creation, async prompt submission, assistant completion detection, and cleanup for bounded canary/design interactions only. No refactor, execution enablement, approval consumption, disable-switch clearance, runner-start contact, OpenCode start, prompt wait call, or code modification is authorized by this reconciliation.

packets/FP-MCP-277.md:11:FP-MCP-277 created canonical checklist artifacts at runs/FP-MCP-277/fp027-master-checklist.md and runs/FP-MCP-277/fp027-master-checklist.json. The checklist is derived from admitted evidence, not from memory or the master plan narrative. It carries forward the exact FP-MCP-268 standing rule: Do not declare FP-027 satisfied by resemblance to any OpenCode HTTP execution path. It requires the guarded runner start path with the original boundary evidence present at the moment of runner contact. The checklist status summary is DONE 12, COMPENSATED 1, BLOCKED 2, NOT_STARTED 14, OPEN 1. It records the current prepared tuple from FP-MCP-276 and states that the next allowed action is operator review of the prepared request and manifest followed by explicit approval or hold/denial. It forbids runner start contact, OpenCode start, prompt submission, wait calls, approval consumption, and disable-switch changes before the missing gates are satisfied. It also records that FP-MCP-278 should refresh the master plan as an orientation document that points to the ledger and checklist rather than becoming a competing current-state source.
packets/FP-MCP-277.md:16:FP-MCP-277 reconstructs the FP-027 master checklist from admitted evidence, preserves the FP-MCP-268 standing rule verbatim, and restores the checklist as the steering artifact: plan orients, ledger proves, checklist steers.

runs/FP-MCP-158/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T082157887Z-3130ce56-result.json:8:  "command": "set -e\ncat > docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot Progress Checklist\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis checklist is an evidence ledger index for ForgePilot V1 progress.\n\nIt answers:\n\n```text\nWhat exists, what proves it, what remains incomplete, and what must not be inferred yet?\n```\n\nThis checklist is not:\n\n- a roadmap,\n- a dashboard,\n- an implementation backlog,\n- a routing plan,\n- a model scorecard,\n- a replacement for packet evidence,\n- a machine-readable source of truth.\n\nThe source of truth remains committed packet artifacts, run artifacts, migrations, tests, and supported read-only query tools.\n\n## Update Rule\n\nNever mark something complete because we believe it works.\n\nMark it complete only because committed packet evidence proves it.\n\nRecommended update rhythm:\n\n```text\npacket implementation/evidence commit\n→ verification evidence recorded\n→ checklist updated only after the evidence boundary is crossed\n```\n\n## Status Vocabulary\n\n| Status | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `NOT_STARTED` | No packet or committed implementation evidence exists yet. |\n| `PACKET_RECORDED` | A packet exists, but implementation or verification evidence is not complete. |\n| `IMPLEMENTED` | Implementation exists in committed code, but verification or direct proof is incomplete. |\n| `VERIFIED` | Verification passed and is recorded, but a required direct MCP or operational proof is not complete. |\n| `DIRECT_MCP_PROVEN` | The capability was proven through the MCP tool surface where applicable. |\n| `RECORDED` | Evidence was recorded in committed run artifacts. |\n| `COMPLETE` | Packet exists, implementation/evidence is committed, verification passed, required direct proof passed, and repos were clean after recording. |\n| `PARTIAL` | Some evidence exists, but one or more required V1 criteria remain incomplete. |\n| `BLOCKED` | Progress is blocked by an external/manual/system condition. |\n| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed and not treated as a failure. |\n\n## ForgePilot V1 Completion Definition\n\nForgePilot V1 is complete when packet intent, execution, telemetry, verification, audit, admission, comparison, and reporting can be reconstructed from committed evidence and supported read-only queries, without relying on private chat memory.\n\nV1 is not complete merely because individual components exist.\n\nV1 is complete when a new operator or agent can inspect the repository and supported query surfaces and reconstruct what happened without relying on a previous ChatGPT session.\n\n## Current Baseline\n\nLast updated by:\n\n- packet: `FP-MCP-158`\n- ForgePilot branch: `main`\n- ForgePilot baseline before checklist implementation: `2d79a9a`\n- bridge branch: `feature/oauth-auth0`\n- bridge baseline: `3a16d68`\n\nRecent closed telemetry/query chain:\n\n- `FP-MCP-153` bounded OpenCode worker invocation\n- `FP-MCP-154` normalized worker telemetry capture\n- `FP-MCP-156` worker telemetry persistence\n- `FP-MCP-157` persisted telemetry query visibility\n\n## V1 Capability Checklist\n\n| Capability | Status | Evidence Packet(s) | Evidence / Notes | Boundary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Packet lifecycle | `PARTIAL` | earlier packets, FP-MCP series | Packets are committed and used as work units. Full V1 lifecycle summary still needs a supported reporting view. | Packet existence is not execution evidence. |\n| Worker lifecycle | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded OpenCode worker invocation has direct MCP proof and committed worker lifecycle artifacts. | Worker lifecycle proof does not imply routing or autonomous execution. |\n| Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-154` | Normalized non-admitted worker telemetry capture exists and is directly readable. | Telemetry is observation, not admitted evidence. |\n| Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-156` | SQLite persistence exists, direct persist/read proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md`. | Persistence does not admit telemetry or make it routing input. |\n| Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Query tool implemented, verified, direct MCP query proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md`. | Querying makes telemetry visible, not meaningful. |\n| Verification evidence | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP evidence chain, earlier FP packets | Typecheck/build/test evidence exists for recent packets, but V1-wide verification reporting is not yet consolidated. | Passing verification is not admission. |\n| Audit evidence | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark/evaluation packets | Audit workflow exists in earlier ForgePilot work, but current FP-MCP chain does not yet expose a consolidated audit query/report view. | Audit is not comparison or admission by itself. |\n| Admission state | `PARTIAL` | FP-009, FP-010, FP-MCP-156/157 | Admission-state concepts and persistence exist; worker telemetry remains `NOT_EVALUATED`. V1 still needs a consolidated admission/reporting path. | `NOT_EVALUATED` must not be treated as admitted evidence. |\n| Comparison result | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark packets | Comparison artifacts exist in earlier evaluation workflows. V1 still needs a supported way to reconstruct comparison state from committed evidence/read-only queries. | Comparison must use admitted evidence only where evidence claims are made. |\n| Reporting / summary view | `NOT_STARTED` | none yet | No supported V1 report or summary view exists for packet/project state. | Manual raw-file inspection is not a V1 reporting surface. |\n| Progress checklist | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | This document creates the first repo-tracked V1 progress checklist. | Checklist is an index, not evidence itself. |\n| Completion criteria | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | V1 completion definition is recorded in this document. | Completion criteria do not imply completion. |\n| Safety boundary documentation | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP packets, boundary docs | Safety boundaries are repeatedly documented in packets and evidence. V1 still needs consolidated boundary reporting or index. | Safety statements must map back to enforced gates or evidence. |\n| Manual MCP Actions refresh process | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Direct query proof records that new MCP tools require manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh before ChatGPT can discover them. | Bridge restart alone does not refresh ChatGPT action schema. |\n| SQLite checklist persistence | `DEFERRED` | `FP-MCP-158` | Markdown is intentionally used for v1 checklist. SQLite persistence may come later after vocabulary stabilizes. | Do not over-engineer checklist state before manual use proves shape. |\n| Routing | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | No model routing or optimization may be inferred. |\n| Model scoring/ranking | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Query results are not model-quality evidence. |\n| Dashboard UI | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Reporting can be CLI/docs before dashboard UI. |\n\n## Recent Packet Evidence Index\n\n| Packet | Capability | Status | ForgePilot Commit(s) | Bridge Commit(s) | Direct MCP Proof | Evidence Artifact(s) | Caveat |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded worker invocation | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | worker lifecycle artifacts under `runs/FP-MCP-153/` | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-154` | Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | telemetry artifacts under worker run directories | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-156` | Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `e3702da`, `09c5094`, `d67c0cc`, `d1b03d5` | `5c2e7af`, `dc1a412` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md` | persisted telemetry remains non-admitted |\n| `FP-MCP-157` | Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `dafa608`, `3b9d6e8`, `e91c91c`, `1e04c7f` | `3a16d68` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md` | manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh required for new MCP tools |\n| `FP-MCP-158` | Progress checklist v1 | `COMPLETE` | this packet | none | not applicable | `docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md` | documentation-only checklist |\n\n## Incomplete V1 Capability Notes\n\n### Reporting / Summary View\n\nStatus: `NOT_STARTED`\n\nRequired before V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect packet state without manually reading raw artifacts.\n- The reporting surface must distinguish observation, verification, audit, admission, and comparison.\n- Any evidence claims must clearly identify whether they are admitted or merely observed.\n\nNon-goals for initial reporting:\n\n- dashboard UI,\n- model ranking,\n- routing recommendations,\n- score calculation.\n\n### Verification Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Individual packets record typecheck/build/test/smoke evidence.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to summarize verification state across packets.\n- Clear distinction between verification pass and admission.\n\n### Audit Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Earlier ForgePilot benchmark/evaluation workflows include audit artifacts.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect audit state across packets.\n- A clear index from packet to audit artifact to final outcome.\n\n### Admission / Comparison Reconstruction\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Admission-state vocabulary and persistence exist in earlier work.\n- Recent telemetry persistence records `admission_state` as `NOT_EVALUATED`.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported read-only way to reconstruct admission/comparison state for a packet.\n- A reporting path that refuses to treat non-admitted observations as evidence.\n\n## Standing Operational Caveats\n\n### Manual OpenAI Actions Schema Refresh\n\nNew MCP tools are not discoverable by ChatGPT until the OpenAI Actions schema is manually refreshed from the Actions page.\n\nObserved in:\n\n- `FP-MCP-157`\n\nOperational implication:\n\n```text\nbridge implementation commit\n→ bridge build\n→ bridge service restart\n→ manual OpenAI Actions refresh\n→ direct ChatGPT MCP proof\n```\n\n### Telemetry Visibility Boundary\n\nTelemetry query visibility does not make telemetry meaningful.\n\nPersisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\n\n### Checklist Boundary\n\nThis checklist is not proof by itself.\n\nA checklist entry is trustworthy only to the extent that it points to committed packet evidence.\n\n## Deferred Ideas\n\nThese may be considered after V1 evidence/reporting surfaces stabilize:\n\n- SQLite-backed progress checklist persistence,\n- automated checklist generation,\n- dashboard UI,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- routing policy,\n- model scoring/ranking.\nEOF\n\ncat > runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt <<'EOF'\nFP-MCP-158 verification\n\nChecklist file exists:\nPASS\n- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md\n\nPacket file exists:\nPASS\n- packets/FP-MCP-158.md\n\nDocumentation-only scope:\nPASS\n- no implementation code files changed\n\nRequired content:\nPASS\n- status vocabulary present\n- V1 completion definition present\n- recent FP-MCP packet evidence index present\n- incomplete V1 capability entries present\n- manual OpenAI Actions refresh caveat present\n- checklist boundary recorded\n\nFinal cleanup:\nPASS pending final commit/status check\nEOF\n\ngit diff -- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt\ngit status --short",

Full V1 lifecycle summary still needs a supported reporting view. | Packet existence is not execution evidence. |\n| Worker lifecycle | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded OpenCode worker invocation has direct MCP proof and committed worker lifecycle artifacts. | Worker lifecycle proof does not imply routing or autonomous execution. |\n| Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-154` | Normalized non-admitted worker telemetry capture exists and is directly readable. | Telemetry is observation, not admitted evidence. |\n| Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-156` | SQLite persistence exists, direct persist/read proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md`. | Persistence does not admit telemetry or make it routing input. |\n| Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Query tool implemented, verified, direct MCP query proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md`. | Querying makes telemetry visible, not meaningful. |\n| Verification evidence | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP evidence chain, earlier FP packets | Typecheck/build/test evidence exists for recent packets, but V1-wide verification reporting is not yet consolidated. | Passing verification is not admission. |\n| Audit evidence | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark/evaluation packets | Audit workflow exists in earlier ForgePilot work, but current FP-MCP chain does not yet expose a consolidated audit query/report view. | Audit is not comparison or admission by itself. |\n| Admission state | `PARTIAL` | FP-009, FP-010, FP-MCP-156/157 | Admission-state concepts and persistence exist; worker telemetry remains `NOT_EVALUATED`. V1 still needs a consolidated admission/reporting path. | `NOT_EVALUATED` must not be treated as admitted evidence. |\n| Comparison result | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark packets | Comparison artifacts exist in earlier evaluation workflows. V1 still needs a supported way to reconstruct comparison state from committed evidence/read-only queries. | Comparison must use admitted evidence only where evidence claims are made. |\n| Reporting / summary view | `NOT_STARTED` | none yet | No supported V1 report or summary view exists for packet/project state. | Manual raw-file inspection is not a V1 reporting surface. |\n| Progress checklist | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | This document creates the first repo-tracked V1 progress checklist. | Checklist is an index, not evidence itself. |\n| Completion criteria | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | V1 completion definition is recorded in this document. | Completion criteria do not imply completion. |\n| Safety boundary documentation | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP packets, boundary docs | Safety boundaries are repeatedly documented in packets and evidence. V1 still needs consolidated boundary reporting or index. | Safety statements must map back to enforced gates or evidence. |\n| Manual MCP Actions refresh process | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Direct query proof records that new MCP tools require manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh before ChatGPT can discover them. | Bridge restart alone does not refresh ChatGPT action schema. |\n| SQLite checklist persistence | `DEFERRED` | `FP-MCP-158` | Markdown is intentionally used for v1 checklist. SQLite persistence may come later after vocabulary stabilizes. | Do not over-engineer checklist state before manual use proves shape. |\n| Routing | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | No model routing or optimization may be inferred. |\n| Model scoring/ranking | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Query results are not model-quality evidence. |\n| Dashboard UI | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Reporting can be CLI/docs before dashboard UI. |\n\n## Recent Packet Evidence Index\n\n| Packet | Capability | Status | ForgePilot Commit(s) | Bridge Commit(s) | Direct MCP Proof | Evidence Artifact(s) | Caveat |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded worker invocation | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | worker lifecycle artifacts under `runs/FP-MCP-153/` | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-154` | Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | telemetry artifacts under worker run directories | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-156` | Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `e3702da`, `09c5094`, `d67c0cc`, `d1b03d5` | `5c2e7af`, `dc1a412` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md` | persisted telemetry remains non-admitted |\n| `FP-MCP-157` | Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `dafa608`, `3b9d6e8`, `e91c91c`, `1e04c7f` | `3a16d68` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md` | manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh required for new MCP tools |\n| `FP-MCP-158` | Progress checklist v1 | `COMPLETE` | this packet | none | not applicable | `docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md` | documentation-only checklist |\n\n## Incomplete V1 Capability Notes\n\n### Reporting / Summary View\n\nStatus: `NOT_STARTED`\n\nRequired before V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect packet state without manually reading raw artifacts.\n- The reporting surface must distinguish observation, verification, audit, admission, and comparison.\n- Any evidence claims must clearly identify whether they are admitted or merely observed.\n\nNon-goals for initial reporting:\n\n- dashboard UI,\n- model ranking,\n- routing recommendations,\n- score calculation.\n\n### Verification Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Individual packets record typecheck/build/test/smoke evidence.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to summarize verification state across packets.\n- Clear distinction between verification pass and admission.\n\n### Audit Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Earlier ForgePilot benchmark/evaluation workflows include audit artifacts.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect audit state across packets.\n- A clear index from packet to audit artifact to final outcome.\n\n### Admission / Comparison Reconstruction\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Admission-state vocabulary and persistence exist in earlier work.\n- Recent telemetry persistence records `admission_state` as `NOT_EVALUATED`.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported read-only way to reconstruct admission/comparison state for a packet.\n- A reporting path that refuses to treat non-admitted observations as evidence.\n\n## Standing Operational Caveats\n\n### Manual OpenAI Actions Schema Refresh\n\nNew MCP tools are not discoverable by ChatGPT until the OpenAI Actions schema is manually refreshed from the Actions page.\n\nObserved in:\n\n- `FP-MCP-157`\n\nOperational implication:\n\n```text\nbridge implementation commit\n→ bridge build\n→ bridge service restart\n→ manual OpenAI Actions refresh\n→ direct ChatGPT MCP proof\n```\n\n### Telemetry Visibility Boundary\n\nTelemetry query visibility does not make telemetry meaningful.\n\nPersisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\n\n### Checklist Boundary\n\nThis checklist is not proof by itself.\n\nA checklist entry is trustworthy only to the extent that it points to committed packet evidence.\n\n## Deferred Ideas\n\nThese may be considered after V1 evidence/reporting surfaces stabilize:\n\n- SQLite-backed progress checklist persistence,\n- automated checklist generation,\n- dashboard UI,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- routing policy,\n- model scoring/ranking.\nEOF\n\ncat > runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt <<'EOF'\nFP-MCP-158 verification\n\nChecklist file exists:\nPASS\n- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md\n\nPacket file exists:\nPASS\n- packets/FP-MCP-158.md\n\nDocumentation-only scope:\nPASS\n- no implementation code files changed\n\nRequired content:\nPASS\n- status vocabulary present\n- V1 completion definition present\n- recent FP-MCP packet evidence index present\n- incomplete V1 capability entries present\n- manual OpenAI Actions refresh caveat present\n- checklist boundary recorded\n\nFinal cleanup:\nPASS pending final commit/status check\nEOF\n\ngit diff -- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt\ngit status --short",

runs/FP-MCP-289/result-summary.md:11:FP-MCP-289 records the auditor-requested reconciliation before any MCP server refactor proceeds. It accounts for admitted evidence IDs 153 through 163, clarifies that FP-MCP-278 became the observed MCP refactor workload decision while FP-MCP-279 performed the master-plan orientation refresh, clarifies the FP-MCP-277 COMPENSATED checklist item, and corrects the direct-HTTP pivot language. The remote-runner guarded-start path retains safety properties that the direct HTTP path has not yet proven: process/state snapshots at contact boundary, live abort-readiness equivalent to process-group abort, no-retry/no-chaining state-machine evidence, approval-consumption coupling, and FP-027 canary discipline. The direct HTTP path has proven authenticated reachability, session creation, async prompt submission, assistant completion detection, and cleanup for bounded canary/design interactions only. No refactor, execution enablement, approval consumption, disable-switch clearance, runner-start contact, OpenCode start, prompt wait call, or code modification is authorized by this reconciliation.

docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot Progress Checklist\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis checklist is an evidence ledger index for ForgePilot V1 progress.\n\nIt answers:\n\n```text\nWhat exists, what proves it, what remains incomplete, and what must not be inferred yet?\n```\n\nThis checklist is not:\n\n- a roadmap,\n- a dashboard,\n- an implementation backlog,\n- a routing plan,\n- a model scorecard,\n- a replacement for packet evidence,\n- a machine-readable source of truth.\n\nThe source of truth remains committed packet artifacts, run artifacts, migrations, tests, and supported read-only query tools.\n\n## Update Rule\n\nNever mark something complete because we believe it works.\n\nMark it complete only because committed packet evidence proves it.\n\nRecommended update rhythm:\n\n```text\npacket implementation/evidence commit\n→ verification evidence recorded\n→ checklist updated only after the evidence boundary is crossed\n```\n\n## Status Vocabulary\n\n| Status | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `NOT_STARTED` | No packet or committed implementation evidence exists yet. |\n| `PACKET_RECORDED` | A packet exists, but implementation or verification evidence is not complete. |\n| `IMPLEMENTED` | Implementation exists in committed code, but verification or direct proof is incomplete. |\n| `VERIFIED` | Verification passed and is recorded, but a required direct MCP or operational proof is not complete. |\n| `DIRECT_MCP_PROVEN` | The capability was proven through the MCP tool surface where applicable. |\n| `RECORDED` | Evidence was recorded in committed run artifacts. |\n| `COMPLETE` | Packet exists, implementation/evidence is committed, verification passed, required direct proof passed, and repos were clean after recording. |\n| `PARTIAL` | Some evidence exists, but one or more required V1 criteria remain incomplete. |\n| `BLOCKED` | Progress is blocked by an external/manual/system condition. |\n| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed and not treated as a failure. |\n\n## ForgePilot V1 Completion Definition\n\nForgePilot V1 is complete when packet intent, execution, telemetry, verification, audit, admission, comparison, and reporting can be reconstructed from committed evidence and supported read-only queries, without relying on private chat memory.\n\nV1 is not complete merely because individual components exist.\n\nV1 is complete when a new operator or agent can inspect the repository and supported query surfaces and reconstruct what happened without relying on a previous ChatGPT session.\n\n## Current Baseline\n\nLast updated by:\n\n- packet: `FP-MCP-158`\n- ForgePilot branch: `main`\n- ForgePilot baseline before checklist implementation: `2d79a9a`\n- bridge branch: `feature/oauth-auth0`\n- bridge baseline: `3a16d68`\n\nRecent closed telemetry/query chain:\n\n- `FP-MCP-153` bounded OpenCode worker invocation\n- `FP-MCP-154` normalized worker telemetry capture\n- `FP-MCP-156` worker telemetry persistence\n- `FP-MCP-157` persisted telemetry query visibility\n\n## V1 Capability Checklist\n\n| Capability | Status | Evidence Packet(s) | Evidence / Notes | Boundary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Packet lifecycle | `PARTIAL` | earlier packets, FP-MCP series | Packets are committed and used as work units. V1 still needs a consolidated admission/reporting path. | `NOT_EVALUATED` must not be treated as admitted evidence. |\n| Comparison result | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark packets | Comparison artifacts exist in earlier evaluation workflows. Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `NOT_STARTED` | No packet or committed implementation evidence exists yet. |\n| `PACKET_RECORDED` | A packet exists, but implementation or verification evidence is not complete. |\n| `IMPLEMENTED` | Implementation exists in committed code, but verification or direct proof is incomplete. |\n| `VERIFIED` | Verification passed and is recorded, but a required direct MCP or operational proof is not complete. |\n| `DIRECT_MCP_PROVEN` | The capability was proven through the MCP tool surface where applicable. |\n| `RECORDED` | Evidence was recorded in committed run artifacts. |\n| `COMPLETE` | Packet exists, implementation/evidence is committed, verification passed, required direct proof passed, and repos were clean after recording. |\n| `PARTIAL` | Some evidence exists, but one or more required V1 criteria remain incomplete. |\n| `BLOCKED` | Progress is blocked by an external/manual/system condition. |\n| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed and not treated as a failure. |\n\n## ForgePilot V1 Completion Definition\n\nForgePilot V1 is complete when packet intent, execution, telemetry, verification, audit, admission, comparison, and reporting can be reconstructed from committed evidence and supported read-only queries, without relying on private chat memory.\n\nV1 is not complete merely because individual components exist.\n\nV1 is complete when a new operator or agent can inspect the repository and supported query surfaces and reconstruct what happened without relying on a previous ChatGPT session.\n\n## Current Baseline\n\nLast updated by:\n\n- packet: `FP-MCP-158`\n- ForgePilot branch: `main`\n- ForgePilot baseline before checklist implementation: `2d79a9a`\n- bridge branch: `feature/oauth-auth0`\n- bridge baseline: `3a16d68`\n\nRecent closed telemetry/query chain:\n\n- `FP-MCP-153` bounded OpenCode worker invocation\n- `FP-MCP-154` normalized worker telemetry capture\n- `FP-MCP-156` worker telemetry persistence\n- `FP-MCP-157` persisted telemetry query visibility\n\n## V1 Capability Checklist\n\n| Capability | Status | Evidence Packet(s) | Evidence / Notes | Boundary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Packet lifecycle | `PARTIAL` | earlier packets, FP-MCP series | Packets are committed and used as work units. Full V1 lifecycle summary still needs a supported reporting view. | Packet existence is not execution evidence. |\n| Worker lifecycle | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded OpenCode worker invocation has direct MCP proof and committed worker lifecycle artifacts. | Worker lifecycle proof does not imply routing or autonomous execution. |\n| Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-154` | Normalized non-admitted worker telemetry capture exists and is directly readable. | Telemetry is observation, not admitted evidence. |\n| Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-156` | SQLite persistence exists, direct persist/read proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md`. | Persistence does not admit telemetry or make it routing input. |\n| Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Query tool implemented, verified, direct MCP query proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md`. | Querying makes telemetry visible, not meaningful. |\n| Verification evidence | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP evidence chain, earlier FP packets | Typecheck/build/test evidence exists for recent packets, but V1-wide verification reporting is not yet consolidated. | Passing verification is not admission. |\n| Audit evidence | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark/evaluation packets | Audit workflow exists in earlier ForgePilot work, but current FP-MCP chain does not yet expose a consolidated audit query/report view. | Audit is not comparison or admission by itself. |\n| Admission state | `PARTIAL` | FP-009, FP-010, FP-MCP-156/157 | Admission-state concepts and persistence exist; worker telemetry remains `NOT_EVALUATED`. V1 still needs a consolidated admission/reporting path. | `NOT_EVALUATED` must not be treated as admitted evidence. |\n| Comparison result | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark packets | Comparison artifacts exist in earlier evaluation workflows. V1 still needs a supported way to reconstruct comparison state from committed evidence/read-only queries. | Comparison must use admitted evidence only where evidence claims are made. |\n| Reporting / summary view | `NOT_STARTED` | none yet | No supported V1 report or summary view exists for packet/project state. | Manual raw-file inspection is not a V1 reporting surface. |\n| Progress checklist | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | This document creates the first repo-tracked V1 progress checklist. | Checklist is an index, not evidence itself. |\n| Completion criteria | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | V1 completion definition is recorded in this document. | Completion criteria do not imply completion. |\n| Safety boundary documentation | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP packets, boundary docs | Safety boundaries are repeatedly documented in packets and evidence. V1 still needs consolidated boundary reporting or index. | Safety statements must map back to enforced gates or evidence. |\n| Manual MCP Actions refresh process | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Direct query proof records that new MCP tools require manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh before ChatGPT can discover them. | Bridge restart alone does not refresh ChatGPT action schema. |\n| SQLite checklist persistence | `DEFERRED` | `FP-MCP-158` | Markdown is intentionally used for v1 checklist. SQLite persistence may come later after vocabulary stabilizes. | Do not over-engineer checklist state before manual use proves shape. |\n| Routing | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | No model routing or optimization may be inferred. |\n| Model scoring/ranking | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Query results are not model-quality evidence. |\n| Dashboard UI | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Reporting can be CLI/docs before dashboard UI. |\n\n## Recent Packet Evidence Index\n\n| Packet | Capability | Status | ForgePilot Commit(s) | Bridge Commit(s) | Direct MCP Proof | Evidence Artifact(s) | Caveat |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded worker invocation | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | worker lifecycle artifacts under `runs/FP-MCP-153/` | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-154` | Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | telemetry artifacts under worker run directories | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-156` | Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `e3702da`, `09c5094`, `d67c0cc`, `d1b03d5` | `5c2e7af`, `dc1a412` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md` | persisted telemetry remains non-admitted |\n| `FP-MCP-157` | Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `dafa608`, `3b9d6e8`, `e91c91c`, `1e04c7f` | `3a16d68` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md` | manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh required for new MCP tools |\n| `FP-MCP-158` | Progress checklist v1 | `COMPLETE` | this packet | none | not applicable | `docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md` | documentation-only checklist |\n\n## Incomplete V1 Capability Notes\n\n### Reporting / Summary View\n\nStatus: `NOT_STARTED`\n\nRequired before V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect packet state without manually reading raw artifacts.\n- The reporting surface must distinguish observation, verification, audit, admission, and comparison.\n- Any evidence claims must clearly identify whether they are admitted or merely observed.\n\nNon-goals for initial reporting:\n\n- dashboard UI,\n- model ranking,\n- routing recommendations,\n- score calculation.\n\n### Verification Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Individual packets record typecheck/build/test/smoke evidence.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to summarize verification state across packets.\n- Clear distinction between verification pass and admission.\n\n### Audit Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Earlier ForgePilot benchmark/evaluation workflows include audit artifacts.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect audit state across packets.\n- A clear index from packet to audit artifact to final outcome.\n\n### Admission / Comparison Reconstruction\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Admission-state vocabulary and persistence exist in earlier work.\n- Recent telemetry persistence records `admission_state` as `NOT_EVALUATED`.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported read-only way to reconstruct admission/comparison state for a packet.\n- A reporting path that refuses to treat non-admitted observations as evidence.\n\n## Standing Operational Caveats\n\n### Manual OpenAI Actions Schema Refresh\n\nNew MCP tools are not discoverable by ChatGPT until the OpenAI Actions schema is manually refreshed from the Actions page.\n\nObserved in:\n\n- `FP-MCP-157`\n\nOperational implication:\n\n```text\nbridge implementation commit\n→ bridge build\n→ bridge service restart\n→ manual OpenAI Actions refresh\n→ direct ChatGPT MCP proof\n```\n\n### Telemetry Visibility Boundary\n\nTelemetry query visibility does not make telemetry meaningful.\n\nPersisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\n\n### Checklist Boundary\n\nThis checklist is not proof by itself.\n\nA checklist entry is trustworthy only to the extent that it points to committed packet evidence.\n\n## Deferred Ideas\n\nThese may be considered after V1 evidence/reporting surfaces stabilize:\n\n- SQLite-backed progress checklist persistence,\n- automated checklist generation,\n- dashboard UI,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- routing policy,\n- model scoring/ranking.\nEOF\n\ncat > runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt <<'EOF'\nFP-MCP-158 verification\n\nChecklist file exists:\nPASS\n- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md\n\nPacket file present\n- recent FP-MCP packet evidence index present\n- incomplete V1 capability entries present\n- manual OpenAI Actions refresh caveat present\n- checklist boundary recorded\n\nFinal cleanup:\nPASS pending final commit/status check\nEOF\n\ngit diff -- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt\ngit status --short",
