docs/releases runs/V1-OBSERVATION runs/FP-OPS-005\ncat > docs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot V1.0 Release Record\n\n## Release\n\nForgePilot V1.0 is complete.\n\n## Completion Evidence\n\n| Field | Value |\n|---|---|\n| V1 completion commit | `54b08f8` |\n| Completion packet | `FP-META-025` |\n| Final project report | `runs/FP-META-025/final-v1-project-report.json` |\n| V1 status | `COMPLETE` |\n| V1 reason | `All non-deferred checklist capabilities are marked COMPLETE.` |\n| Complete capabilities | `27` |\n| Deferred capabilities | `4` |\n| Incomplete capabilities | `0` |\n\n## V1 Meaning\n\nForgePilot V1.0 means:\n\n```text\nPacket intent, execution evidence, telemetry/observations, verification, audit, admission state, comparison state, and reporting can be reconstructed from committed evidence and supported read-only reports.\n```\n\n## V1 Non-Goals\n\nForgePilot V1.0 does not mean:\n\n- routing is implemented,\n- model scoring/ranking is implemented,\n- dashboard UI is implemented,\n- SQLite checklist persistence is implemented,\n- all evidence is admitted,\n- all observations are routing input,\n- V2 has started.\n\n## Deferred Capabilities\n\nThe following are intentionally deferred and do not block V1.0:\n\n- SQLite checklist persistence,\n- routing,\n- model scoring/ranking,\n- dashboard UI.\n\n## V1 Operating Posture\n\nV1.0 should now be used before V2 is proposed.\n\nThe purpose of the next period is to observe V1 under real use and record whether gaps are V1.x refinements or true V2 triggers.\n\n## Boundary\n\nThis release record is an index over committed evidence.\n\nIt does not create evidence, admission, routing authority, model scoring, or V2 scope.\nEOF\ncat > docs/v1-observation-plan.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot V1 Observation Plan\n\n## Purpose\n\nUse ForgePilot V1.0 in real work before deciding whether V1.x or V2 is justified.\n\nV2 must be evidence-triggered, not ambition-triggered.\n\n## Observation Rule\n\nDo not propose V2 from imagination.\n\nUse V1, record friction, and decide from evidence.\n\n## Observation Log\n\nOperational observations should be recorded in:\n\n```text\nruns/V1-OBSERVATION/log.md\n```\n\n## Observation Questions\n\nFor each real ForgePilot work session, record whether:\n\n1. `project-report` was enough to know current project state.\n2. `project-report` was enough to choose next work.\n3. `packet-report` was enough to inspect packet evidence.\n4. `reconstruction-report` was enough to inspect verification/audit/admission/comparison state.\n5. The packet status index helped with historical packet navigation.\n6. The safety boundary index prevented overclaiming.\n7. The lifecycle index clarified packet state.\n8. Private chat memory was still needed.\n9. Manual grep/find was still needed.\n10. The checklist felt trustworthy as an index.\n11. Any report overclaimed.\n12. Any report under-reported useful evidence.\n13. Any step was confusing or repetitive.\n14. Any deferred capability became repeatedly necessary.\n\n## Friction Categories\n\nUse these categories in observations:\n\n| Category | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `REPORT_FORMATTING` | Information exists but is hard to read. |\n| `REPORT_MISSING_FIELD` | Report lacks a useful field. |\n| `REPORT_OVERCLAIM` | Report states more than evidence supports. |\n| `REPORT_UNDERCLAIM` | Report hides useful evidence that exists. |\n| `MANUAL_SEARCH_REQUIRED` | Operator needed grep/find/manual inspection. |\n| `PRIVATE_MEMORY_REQUIRED` | Operator needed chat memory not in repo evidence. |\n| `MCP_EXPOSURE_NEEDED` | Existing behavior needs MCP access. |\n| `PERSISTENCE_NEEDED` | File/checklist state should move to structured persistence. |\n| `ADMISSION_FIRST_REPORTING_NEEDED` | Reports need admitted-evidence-first views. |\n| `ROUTING_PRESSURE` | User/operator needed routing/model choice from evidence. |\n| `SCORING_PRESSURE` | User/operator needed model scoring/ranking. |\n| `UI_PRESSURE` | CLI/docs reports became insufficient. |\n\n## V1.x Triggers\n\nUse V1.x for improvements that preserve the V1 contract.\n\nExamples:\n\n- better report formatting,\n- more report fields,\n- clearer docs,\n- more packet status index entries,\n- command aliases,\n- MCP exposure for existing read-only reports,\n- additional smoke tests,\n- better operator guide.\n\n## V2 Triggers\n\nV2 may be proposed only after repeated observations show that V1 cannot support the next required class of work without changing the contract.\n\nValid V2 triggers include repeated need for:\n\n- persisted checklist/query state,\n- admitted-evidence-first reporting,\n- routing decisions from admitted evidence,\n- model scoring/ranking,\n- dashboard/UI because CLI/docs reports are insufficient,\n- multi-run statistical observatory behavior,\n- architectural changes that cannot be handled as V1.x refinements.\n\n## Non-Triggers for V2\n\nThe following do not justify V2 by themselves:\n\n- report formatting improvements,\n- doc cleanup,\n- command aliases,\n- missing convenience fields,\n- incomplete packet status index coverage,\n- convenient MCP exposure for existing behavior,\n- one-off operator confusion.\n\n## Review Condition\n\nReview the observation log after enough real V1 usage exists to show a pattern.\n\nA pattern means repeated observations, not a single inconvenience.\n\n## Boundary\n\nThe observation plan collects evidence for future planning.\n\nIt does not start V2, define V2 architecture, or authorize routing/model scoring/dashboard work.\nEOF\ncat > runs/V1-OBSERVATION/log.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot V1 Observation Log\n\n## Purpose\n\nRecord operational observations from using ForgePilot V1.0.\n\nThis log is used to decide whether future work belongs in V1.x or requires V2.\n\n## Entry Template\n\n```text\n## OBS-YYYYMMDD-NN — <short title>\n\nDate:\nPacket / work context:\nCommands or reports used:\n\nObservation:\n\nCategory:\n\nEvidence path(s):\n\nImpact:\n\nSuggested classification:\n- V1.x refinement\n- V2 trigger candidate\n- No action\n\nNotes:\n```\n\n## Initial State\n\nForgePilot V1.0 is complete according to:\n\n```text\nruns/FP-META-025/final-v1-project-report.json\n```\n\nNo V1 operational observations have been recorded yet after the release marker.\nEOF\npython - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\np = Path('docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md')\ns = p.read_text()\nrow = '| V1 release and observation plan | `COMPLETE` | `FP-OPS-005` | V1 release record and observation protocol are documented in `docs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md` and `docs/v1-observation-plan.md`. | V2 must be evidence-triggered, not ambition-triggered. |'\nmarker = '| V1 evidence capability completion index | `COMPLETE` | `FP-META-025` | Final V1 evidence capabilities are evaluated in `docs/v1-evidence-capability-completion-index.md`. | Completion is V1-scoped and does not include deferred routing/scoring/dashboard work. |'\nif row not in s and marker in s:\n    s = s.replace(marker, marker + '\\n' + row)\nif 'docs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md' not in s:\n    s += '\\n\\n## V1 Release\\n\\nForgePilot V1.0 release record:\\n\\n```text\\ndocs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md\\n```\\n\\nV1 observation plan:\\n\\n```text\\ndocs/v1-observation-plan.md\\n```\\n\\nObservation log:\\n\\n```text\\nruns/V1-OBSERVATION/log.md\\n```\\n'\np.write_text(s)\nPY\ncat > runs/FP-OPS-005/verification.txt <<'EOF'\nFP-OPS-005 verification\n\nV1 release record exists:\nPASS\n- docs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md\n\nV1 observation plan exists:\nPASS\n- docs/v1-observation-plan.md\n\nV1 observation log exists:\nPASS\n- runs/V1-OBSERVATION/log.md\n\nV1 completion evidence is referenced:\nPASS\n- completion commit: 54b08f8\n- final report: runs/FP-META-025/final-v1-project-report.json\n- status: COMPLETE\n- complete: 27\n- deferred: 4\n- incomplete: 0\n\nV1.x vs V2 decision rules:\nPASS\n- V1.x triggers documented\n- V2 triggers documented\n- non-triggers documented\n- V2 must be evidence-triggered, not ambition-triggered\n\nDocumentation-only scope:\nPASS\n- no implementation code files changed\nEOF\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- status ---'\ngit status --short\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- release grep ---'\ngrep -n \"V1 completion commit\\|V1 Meaning\\|Deferred Capabilities\\|Boundary\" docs/releases/forgepilot-v1.md\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- observation grep ---'\ngrep -n \"V2 Triggers\\|V1.x Triggers\\|Non-Triggers\\|Observation Questions\" docs/v1-observation-plan.md",

runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111335817Z-496ab19f-stdout.log:77:runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111049522Z-0783b01d-result.json:8:  "command": "mkdir -p runs/FP-META-034\n{\n  printf '%s\\n' '# FP-META-034 admission source search'\n  printf '%s\\n' ''\n  printf '%s\\n' '## Git baseline'\n  git branch --show-current\n  git rev-parse --short HEAD\n  git status --short\n  printf '%s\\n' ''\n  printf '%s\\n' '## Search: admission distribution/reporting sources'\n  grep -RInE \"admission_state|evidence_records|evidence_admission_events|admission_invalidation_events|NOT_EVALUATED|ADMITTED|PENDING|REJECTED|QUARANTINED|final-v1-project-report|project-report\" src docs packets runs package.json 2>/dev/null || true\n} > runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt\nnode - <<'NODE'\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst outPath = 'runs/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json';\nconst result = {\n  packet_id: 'FP-META-034',\n  generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n  database_path: 'data/forgepilot.db',\n  method: 'readonly better-sqlite3 queries against current data/forgepilot.db',\n  query_status: 'NOT_RUN',\n  tables: {},\n  distributions: {},\n  notes: []\n};\ntry {\n  const Database = require('better-sqlite3');\n  if (!fs.existsSync('data/forgepilot.db')) {\n    result.query_status = 'DATABASE_NOT_FOUND';\n    fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\n    process.exit(0);\n  }\n  const db = new Database('data/forgepilot.db', { readonly: true });\n  const tables = db.prepare(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name\").all().map(r => r.name);\n  for (const name of ['evidence_records','evidence_admission_events','admission_invalidation_events','model_comparison_events']) {\n    result.tables[name] = tables.includes(name) ? 'PRESENT' : 'ABSENT';\n  }\n  function groupCount(table, col) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT ${col} AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table} GROUP BY ${col} ORDER BY ${col}`).all();\n  }\n  function count(table) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table}`).get().count;\n  }\n  result.query_status = 'OK';\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_total = count('evidence_records');\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_by_admission_state = groupCount('evidence_records', 'admission_state');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_total = count('evidence_admission_events');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_by_decision = tables.includes('evidence_admission_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT admission_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM evidence_admission_events GROUP BY admission_decision ORDER BY admission_decision').all() : null;\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_total = count('admission_invalidation_events');\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_by_decision = tables.includes('admission_invalidation_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT invalidation_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM admission_invalidation_events GROUP BY invalidation_decision ORDER BY invalidation_decision').all() : null;\n  if (tables.includes('evidence_records')) {\n    result.samples = db.prepare('SELECT evidence_id, packet_id, run_id, model_id, model_role, admission_state, validation_state, trust_tier, created_at FROM evidence_records ORDER BY evidence_id LIMIT 50').all();\n  }\n  result.notes.push('Counts are SQLite-derived from current data/forgepilot.db only.');\n  result.notes.push('Model trust labels are intentionally not counted as admission states.');\n  result.notes.push('NOT_EVALUATED is not admitted evidence.');\n  db.close();\n} catch (err) {\n  result.query_status = 'QUERY_FAILED';\n  result.error = String(err && err.stack ? err.stack : err);\n}\nfs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\nNODE\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- query json ---'\ncat runs/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json",

db.prepare('SELECT admission_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM evidence_admission_events GROUP BY admission_decision ORDER BY admission_decision').all() : null;\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_total = count('admission_invalidation_events');\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_by_decision = tables.includes('admission_invalidation_events') ? runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111335817Z-496ab19f-stdout.log:126:runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt:1134:runs/FP-META-032/model-trust-source-search.txt:894:runs/FP-MCP-157/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T070016779Z-b449e31e-request.json:7:  "command": "set -e\ncat > packets/FP-MCP-157.md <<'EOF'\n# FP-MCP-157 — Worker Telemetry Query Tool v1\n\n## Task\n\nExpose a read-only MCP query tool for persisted OpenCode worker telemetry observations.\n\n## Goal\n\nAllow GPT to query persisted worker telemetry rows without mutating telemetry, admitting evidence, scoring models, routing future work, or touching live OpenCode state.\n\nFP-MCP-157 answers one question:\n\n> Can persisted worker telemetry observations be safely queried through MCP?\n\n## Background\n\nFP-MCP-153 introduced bounded OpenCode worker invocation.\n\nFP-MCP-154 introduced normalized worker telemetry capture.\n\nFP-MCP-156 introduced SQLite persistence for worker telemetry observations.\n\nFP-MCP-157 makes those persisted observations visible through a constrained read-only MCP query surface.\n\nThe architectural line is:\n\n```text\ncapture → persist → read one row → query persisted observations\n```\n\nThis packet must not cross into:\n\n- admission,\n- routing,\n- model scoring,\n- worker control,\n- raw OpenCode internals.\n\nFP-MCP-157 should make telemetry visible, not meaningful.\n\nQuerying is observation access. Meaning comes later through admission, reporting, and comparison packets.\n\n## Scope\n\nImplement one read-only MCP tool in:\n\n```text\n/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot-chatgpt-mcp\n```\n\nand any required ForgePilot-side CLI/database query support in:\n\n```text\n/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot\n```\n\n## Required MCP Tool\n\nAdd:\n\n```text\nforgepilot_query_persisted_opencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\n## Required Inputs\n\nAll inputs are optional unless otherwise stated:\n\n- `packetId?: string`\n- `workerStatus?: string`\n- `terminalState?: string`\n- `validationState?: string`\n- `admissionState?: string`\n- `limit?: number`\n\n## Query Semantics\n\nThe tool must query only the persisted SQLite table:\n\n```text\nopencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\nIt must not read live worker artifacts, recapture telemetry, or inspect raw OpenCode state.\n\n## Allowed Filters\n\nThe tool may filter by:\n\n- packet id,\n- worker status,\n- terminal state,\n- validation state,\n- admission state.\n\nThe tool may support a bounded limit.\n\nRecommended default limit:\n\n```text\n20\n```\n\nRecommended maximum limit:\n\n```text\n100\n```\n\n## Required Output Shape\n\nReturn structured JSON similar to:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"rows\": [\n    {\n      \"packet_id\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n      \"worker_id\": \"WORKER-...\",\n      \"worker_status\": \"SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"terminal_state\": \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"trust_tier\": \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\",\n      \"validation_state\": \"VALID\",\n      \"admission_state\": \"NOT_EVALUATED\",\n      \"persisted_at\": \"...\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"count\": 1,\n  \"filters\": {\n    \"packetId\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n    \"validationState\": \"VALID\"\n  },\n  \"limit\": 20,\n  \"observation_semantics\": \"Persisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\"\n}\n```\n\n## Required Row Fields\n\nEach returned row should include at minimum:\n\n- `packet_id`\n- `worker_id`\n- `schema_version`\n- `captured_at`\n- `persisted_at`\n- `worker_status`\n- `terminal_state`\n- `provenance_completeness`\n- `trust_tier`\n- `validation_state`\n- `admission_state`\n- `duration_ms`\n- `target_workspace_id`\n- `model_id`\n- `telemetry_path`\n\nThe tool may omit long fields such as full command args unless explicitly included later by another packet.\n\n## Safety Requirements\n\nThe tool must not:\n\n- mutate SQLite rows,\n- persist new telemetry,\n- recapture telemetry,\n- start workers,\n- stop workers,\n- restart workers,\n- attach to the legacy OpenCode server,\n- read raw OpenCode session databases,\n- infer model quality,\n- rank models,\n- make routing decisions,\n- change admission state,\n- treat query results as admitted evidence.\n\n## Non-Goals\n\nThis packet does not implement:\n\n- evidence admission,\n- routing,\n- model comparison,\n- dashboards,\n- reporting summaries,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- score calculation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- batch backfill,\n- worker lifecycle control.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n1. Build passes.\n2. Query tool is visible after connector refresh.\n3. Query without filters returns persisted telemetry rows up to the default limit.\n4. Query by packet id works.\n5. Query by worker status works.\n6. Query by terminal state works.\n7. Query by validation state works.\n8. Query by admission state works.\n9. Limit is bounded and enforced.\n10. Query result includes `observation_semantics`.\n11. Query result does not mutate the database.\n12. Query result does not start, stop, or inspect live workers.\n13. Existing persisted FP-MCP-153 worker telemetry can be returned.\n14. ForgePilot and bridge repositories are clean after evidence is recorded.\n\n## Verification Requirements\n\nVerify at minimum:\n\n- ForgePilot typecheck,\n- bridge build,\n- query with no filters,\n- query with `packetId = FP-MCP-153`,\n- query with `workerStatus = SUCCEEDED`,\n- query with `terminalState = TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED`,\n- query with `validationState = VALID`,\n- query with `admissionState = NOT_EVALUATED`,\n- bounded limit behavior,\n- direct MCP query after connector refresh,\n- final clean-state check.\n\n## Expected Result\n\nGPT can query persisted worker telemetry observations safely through MCP.\n\nTelemetry becomes visible and inspectable, but not meaningful in the admission, routing, or model-quality sense.\n\nNo aggregation is introduced in this packet. A simple filtered row query is the intended next step.\nEOF\n\ngit status --short packets/FP-MCP-157.md\ngit diff -- packets/FP-MCP-157.md",

Tool\n\nAdd:\n\n```text\nforgepilot_query_persisted_opencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\n## Required Inputs\n\nAll inputs are optional unless otherwise stated:\n\n- `packetId?: string`\n- `workerStatus?: string`\n- `terminalState?: string`\n- `validationState?: string`\n- `admissionState?: string`\n- `limit?: number`\n\n## Query Semantics\n\nThe tool must query only the persisted SQLite table:\n\n```text\nopencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\nIt must not read live worker artifacts, recapture telemetry, or inspect raw OpenCode state.\n\n## Allowed Filters\n\nThe tool may filter by:\n\n- packet id,\n- worker status,\n- terminal state,\n- validation state,\n- admission state.\n\nThe tool may support a bounded limit.\n\nRecommended default limit:\n\n```text\n20\n```\n\nRecommended maximum limit:\n\n```text\n100\n```\n\n## Required Output Shape\n\nReturn structured JSON similar to:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"rows\": [\n    {\n      \"packet_id\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n      \"worker_id\": \"WORKER-...\",\n      \"worker_status\": \"SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"terminal_state\": \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"trust_tier\": \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\",\n      \"validation_state\": \"VALID\",\n      \"admission_state\": \"NOT_EVALUATED\",\n      \"persisted_at\": \"...\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"count\": 1,\n  \"filters\": {\n    \"packetId\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n    \"validationState\": \"VALID\"\n  },\n  \"limit\": 20,\n  \"observation_semantics\": \"Persisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\"\n}\n```\n\n## Required Row Fields\n\nEach returned row should include at minimum:\n\n- `packet_id`\n- `worker_id`\n- `schema_version`\n- `captured_at`\n- `persisted_at`\n- `worker_status`\n- `terminal_state`\n- `provenance_completeness`\n- `trust_tier`\n- `validation_state`\n- `admission_state`\n- `duration_ms`\n- `target_workspace_id`\n- `model_id`\n- `telemetry_path`\n\nThe tool may omit long fields such as full command args unless explicitly included later by another packet.\n\n## Safety Requirements\n\nThe tool must not:\n\n- mutate SQLite rows,\n- persist new telemetry,\n- recapture telemetry,\n- start workers,\n- stop workers,\n- restart workers,\n- attach to the legacy OpenCode server,\n- read raw OpenCode session databases,\n- infer model quality,\n- rank models,\n- make routing decisions,\n- change admission state,\n- treat query results as admitted evidence.\n\n## Non-Goals\n\nThis packet does not implement:\n\n- evidence admission,\n- routing,\n- model comparison,\n- dashboards,\n- reporting summaries,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- score calculation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- batch backfill,\n- worker lifecycle control.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n1. Build passes.\n2. Query tool is visible after connector refresh.\n3. Query without filters returns persisted telemetry rows up to the default limit.\n4. Query by packet id works.\n5. Query by worker status works.\n6. Query by terminal state works.\n7. Query by validation state works.\n8. Query by admission state works.\n9. Limit is bounded and enforced.\n10. Query result includes `observation_semantics`.\n11. Query result does not mutate the database.\n12. Query result does not start, stop, or inspect live workers.\n13. Existing persisted FP-MCP-153 worker telemetry can be returned.\n14. ForgePilot and bridge repositories are clean after evidence is recorded.\n\n## Verification Requirements\n\nVerify at minimum:\n\n- ForgePilot typecheck,\n- bridge build,\n- query with no filters,\n- query with `packetId = FP-MCP-153`,\n- query with `workerStatus = SUCCEEDED`,\n- query with `terminalState = TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED`,\n- query with `validationState = VALID`,\n- query with `admissionState = NOT_EVALUATED`,\n- bounded limit behavior,\n- direct MCP query after connector refresh,\n- final clean-state check.\n\n## Expected Result\n\nGPT can query persisted worker telemetry observations safely through MCP.\n\nTelemetry becomes visible and inspectable, but not meaningful in the admission, routing, or model-quality sense.\n\nNo aggregation is introduced in this packet. A simple filtered row query is the intended next step.\nEOF\n\ngit status --short packets/FP-MCP-157.md\ngit diff -- packets/FP-MCP-157.md",

Meaning comes later through admission, reporting, and comparison packets.\n\n## Scope\n\nImplement one read-only MCP tool in:\n\n```text\n/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot-chatgpt-mcp\n```\n\nand any required ForgePilot-side CLI/database query support in:\n\n```text\n/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot\n```\n\n## Required MCP Tool\n\nAdd:\n\n```text\nforgepilot_query_persisted_opencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\n## Required Inputs\n\nAll inputs are optional unless otherwise stated:\n\n- `packetId?: string`\n- `workerStatus?: string`\n- `terminalState?: string`\n- `validationState?: string`\n- `admissionState?: string`\n- `limit?: number`\n\n## Query Semantics\n\nThe tool must query only the persisted SQLite table:\n\n```text\nopencode_worker_telemetry\n```\n\nIt must not read live worker artifacts, recapture telemetry, or inspect raw OpenCode state.\n\n## Allowed Filters\n\nThe tool may filter by:\n\n- packet id,\n- worker status,\n- terminal state,\n- validation state,\n- admission state.\n\nThe tool may support a bounded limit.\n\nRecommended default limit:\n\n```text\n20\n```\n\nRecommended maximum limit:\n\n```text\n100\n```\n\n## Required Output Shape\n\nReturn structured JSON similar to:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"rows\": [\n    {\n      \"packet_id\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n      \"worker_id\": \"WORKER-...\",\n      \"worker_status\": \"SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"terminal_state\": \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\",\n      \"trust_tier\": \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\",\n      \"validation_state\": \"VALID\",\n      \"admission_state\": \"NOT_EVALUATED\",\n      \"persisted_at\": \"...\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"count\": 1,\n  \"filters\": {\n    \"packetId\": \"FP-MCP-153\",\n    \"validationState\": \"VALID\"\n  },\n  \"limit\": 20,\n  \"observation_semantics\": \"Persisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\"\n}\n```\n\n## Required Row Fields\n\nEach returned row should include at minimum:\n\n- `packet_id`\n- `worker_id`\n- `schema_version`\n- `captured_at`\n- `persisted_at`\n- `worker_status`\n- `terminal_state`\n- `provenance_completeness`\n- `trust_tier`\n- `validation_state`\n- `admission_state`\n- `duration_ms`\n- `target_workspace_id`\n- `model_id`\n- `telemetry_path`\n\nThe tool may omit long fields such as full command args unless explicitly included later by another packet.\n\n## Safety Requirements\n\nThe tool must not:\n\n- mutate SQLite rows,\n- persist new telemetry,\n- recapture telemetry,\n- start workers,\n- stop workers,\n- restart workers,\n- attach to the legacy OpenCode server,\n- read raw OpenCode session databases,\n- infer model quality,\n- rank models,\n- make routing decisions,\n- change admission state,\n- treat query results as admitted evidence.\n\n## Non-Goals\n\nThis packet does not implement:\n\n- evidence admission,\n- routing,\n- model comparison,\n- dashboards,\n- reporting summaries,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- score calculation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- batch backfill,\n- worker lifecycle control.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n1. Build passes.\n2. Query tool is visible after connector refresh.\n3. Query without filters returns persisted telemetry rows up to the default limit.\n4. Query by packet id works.\n5. Query by worker status works.\n6. Query by terminal state works.\n7. Query by validation state works.\n8. Query by admission state works.\n9. Limit is bounded and enforced.\n10. Query result includes `observation_semantics`.\n11. Query result does not mutate the database.\n12. Query result does not start, stop, or inspect live workers.\n13. Existing persisted FP-MCP-153 worker telemetry can be returned.\n14. runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111335817Z-496ab19f-stdout.log:215:runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt:2096:runs/FP-011/audit-deepseek-by-qwen.md:58:**PASS.** The `evidence_record_validation_events` and `evidence_record_admission_events` tables are additive. They reference `evidence_records.evidence_id` via foreign keys (`migrations/007:7`, `migrations/007:28`). They do not replace or alter the `evidence_records` table. FP-010 `getEvidenceRecord` is the sole record-fetch authority (`evidence-record-evaluation.ts:5`, line 245, line 287).

runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111335817Z-496ab19f-stdout.log:234:runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt:3075:runs/FP-MCP-154/terminal-commands/TERM-20260701T203217214Z-4271cddd-result.json:8:  "command": "cat > src/opencode-worker-telemetry.ts <<'EOF'\nimport { createHash } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { readFile, stat, writeFile } from \"node:fs/promises\";\nimport path from \"node:path\";\nimport { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, type WorkerStatus } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\n\nconst FORGEPILOT_REPO = \"/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot\";\nconst RUNS_ROOT = path.join(FORGEPILOT_REPO, \"runs\");\nconst SCHEMA_VERSION = \"forgepilot.opencode-worker-telemetry.v1\";\n\nexport interface CaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryInput {\n  packetId: string;\n  workerId: string;\n  approval: string;\n}\n\nexport type TerminalStateClassification =\n  | \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\"\n  | \"TERMINAL_FAILED\"\n  | \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\"\n  | \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\";\n\nexport interface ArtifactTelemetry {\n  path: string;\n  exists: boolean;\n  byteCount: number | null;\n  sha256: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry {\n  schemaVersion: string;\n  packetId: string;\n  workerId: string;\n  capturedAt: string;\n  workerStatus: WorkerStatus[\"status\"];\n  startedAt: string;\n  completedAt: string | null;\n  durationMs: number | null;\n  targetWorkspaceId: string;\n  workspacePath: string;\n  promptFile: string;\n  promptFileSha256: string;\n  modelId: string | null;\n  command: {\n    executable: string;\n    args: string[];\n    argsCount: number;\n  };\n  artifacts: {\n    status: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    startRequest: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    stdout: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    stderr: ArtifactTelemetry;\n  };\n  classifications: {\n    terminalState: TerminalStateClassification;\n    provenanceCompleteness: \"COMPLETE\" | \"INCOMPLETE\";\n    trustTier: \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\";\n    validationState: \"VALID\" | \"INCOMPLETE\";\n    admissionState: \"NOT_EVALUATED\";\n  };\n  telemetryPath: string;\n  observationSemantics: string;\n  reasons: string[];\n}\n\nfunction workerDir(packetId: string, workerId: string): string {\n  return path.join(RUNS_ROOT, packetId, \"opencode-workers\", workerId);\n}\n\nfunction telemetryPath(packetId: string, workerId: string): string {\n  return path.join(workerDir(packetId, workerId), \"telemetry.json\");\n}\n\nasync function artifactTelemetry(filePath: string): Promise<ArtifactTelemetry> {\n  try {\n    const [s, bytes] = await Promise.all([stat(filePath), readFile(filePath)]);\n    if (!s.isFile()) {\n      return { path: filePath, exists: false, byteCount: null, sha256: null };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      path: filePath,\n      exists: true,\n      byteCount: bytes.byteLength,\n      sha256: createHash(\"sha256\").update(bytes).digest(\"hex\"),\n    };\n  } catch {\n    return { path: filePath, exists: false, byteCount: null, sha256: null };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction durationMs(status: WorkerStatus): number | null {\n  if (status.completedAt === null) return null;\n  const started = Date.parse(status.startedAt);\n  const completed = Date.parse(status.completedAt);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(started) || !Number.isFinite(completed)) return null;\n  return Math.max(0, completed - started);\n}\n\nfunction classifyTerminalState(status: WorkerStatus[\"status\"]): TerminalStateClassification {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"SUCCEEDED\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\";\n    case \"FAILED\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_FAILED\";\n    case \"UNKNOWN_EXIT\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\";\n    case \"RUNNING\":\n      return \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction requiredFieldsPresent(status: WorkerStatus): boolean {\n  return Boolean(\n    status.workerId &&\n      status.packetId &&\n      status.targetWorkspaceId &&\n      status.workspacePath &&\n      status.promptFile &&\n      status.promptFileSha256 &&\n      status.startedAt &&\n      status.statusPath &&\n      status.startRequestPath &&\n      status.stdoutPath &&\n      status.stderrPath\n  );\n}\n\nfunction provenanceComplete(status: WorkerStatus, artifacts: OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry[\"artifacts\"]): boolean {\n  return (\n    requiredFieldsPresent(status) &&\n    artifacts.status.exists &&\n    artifacts.status.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.startRequest.exists &&\n    artifacts.startRequest.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.stdout.exists &&\n    artifacts.stdout.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.stderr.exists &&\n    artifacts.stderr.sha256 !== null\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(input: CaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryInput): Promise<OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry> {\n  if (input.approval !== \"CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY\") {\n    throw new Error(`Approval must be \"CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY\", got \"${input.approval}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const status = await getOpenCodeWorkerStatus(input.packetId, input.workerId);\n  const telemetryFile = telemetryPath(input.packetId, input.workerId);\n\n  const artifacts = {\n    status: await artifactTelemetry(status.statusPath),\n    startRequest: await artifactTelemetry(status.startRequestPath),\n    stdout: await artifactTelemetry(status.stdoutPath),\n    stderr: await artifactTelemetry(status.stderrPath),\n  };\n\n  const complete = provenanceComplete(status, artifacts);\n  const reasons: string[] = [];\n  if (!complete) reasons.push(\"PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE\");\n  if (status.status === \"RUNNING\") reasons.push(\"WORKER_STILL_RUNNING\");\n\n  const telemetry: OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry = {\n    schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,\n    packetId: input.packetId,\n    workerId: input.workerId,\n    capturedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    workerStatus: status.status,\n    startedAt: status.startedAt,\n    completedAt: status.completedAt,\n    durationMs: durationMs(status),\n    targetWorkspaceId: status.targetWorkspaceId,\n    workspacePath: status.workspacePath,\n    promptFile: status.promptFile,\n    promptFileSha256: status.promptFileSha256,\n    modelId: status.modelId,\n    command: {\n      executable: status.command.executable,\n      args: status.command.args,\n      argsCount: status.command.args.length,\n    },\n    artifacts,\n    classifications: {\n      terminalState: classifyTerminalState(status.status),\n      provenanceCompleteness: complete ? \"COMPLETE\" : \"INCOMPLETE\",\n      trustTier: \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\",\n      validationState: complete ? \"VALID\" : \"INCOMPLETE\",\n      admissionState: \"NOT_EVALUATED\",\n    },\n    telemetryPath: telemetryFile,\n    observationSemantics: \"OpenCode worker telemetry is an observation, not admitted evidence or routing input by itself.\",\n    reasons,\n  };\n\n  await writeFile(telemetryFile, JSON.stringify(telemetry, null, 2) + \"\\n\", \"utf8\");\n  return telemetry;\n}\n\nexport async function readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(packetId: string, workerId: string): Promise<OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry> {\n  const filePath = telemetryPath(packetId, workerId);\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(await readFile(filePath, \"utf8\")) as OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry;\n  } catch {\n    throw new Error(`Telemetry artifact does not exist: ${filePath}`);\n  }\n}\nEOF\n\npython3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\np = Path('src/server.ts')\ns = p.read_text()\nif 'opencode-worker-telemetry.js' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('import { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, readOpenCodeWorkerResult, startOpenCodeWorker } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\\n', 'import { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, readOpenCodeWorkerResult, startOpenCodeWorker } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\\nimport { captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry, readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry } from \"./opencode-worker-telemetry.js\";\\n')\n\nschema = '''\nconst ArtifactTelemetrySchema = z.object({\n  path: z.string(),\n  exists: z.boolean(),\n  byteCount: z.number().nullable(),\n  sha256: z.string().nullable()\n});\n\nconst OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema = {\n  schemaVersion: z.string(),\n  packetId: z.string(),\n  workerId: z.string(),\n  capturedAt: z.string(),\n  workerStatus: z.enum([\"RUNNING\", \"SUCCEEDED\", \"FAILED\", \"UNKNOWN_EXIT\"]),\n  startedAt: z.string(),\n  completedAt: z.string().nullable(),\n  durationMs: z.number().nullable(),\n  targetWorkspaceId: z.string(),\n  workspacePath: z.string(),\n  promptFile: z.string(),\n  promptFileSha256: z.string(),\n  modelId: z.string().nullable(),\n  command: z.object({\n    executable: z.string(),\n    args: z.array(z.string()),\n    argsCount: z.number()\n  }),\n  artifacts: z.object({\n    status: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    startRequest: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    stdout: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    stderr: ArtifactTelemetrySchema\n  }),\n  classifications: z.object({\n    terminalState: z.enum([\"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\", \"TERMINAL_FAILED\", \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\", \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\"]),\n    provenanceCompleteness: z.enum([\"COMPLETE\", \"INCOMPLETE\"]),\n    trustTier: z.literal(\"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\"),\n    validationState: z.enum([\"VALID\", \"INCOMPLETE\"]),\n    admissionState: z.literal(\"NOT_EVALUATED\")\n  }),\n  telemetryPath: z.string(),\n  observationSemantics: z.string(),\n  reasons: z.array(z.string())\n};\n\nconst ForgePilotCaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema = OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema;\nconst ForgePilotReadOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema = OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema;\n'''\nif 'OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('const OpenCodeWorkerStatusSchema = {\\n', schema + '\\nconst OpenCodeWorkerStatusSchema = {\\n')\n\ntools = '''\n  mcpServer.registerTool(\n    \"forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry\",\n    {\n      description: \"Capture normalized, non-admitted telemetry for an existing OpenCode worker and write telemetry.json under the worker directory.\",\n      inputSchema: {\n        packetId: z.string().describe(\"Known ForgePilot packet id.\"),\n        workerId: z.string().describe(\"Existing OpenCode worker id.\"),\n        approval: z.string().describe(\"Must exactly equal CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY.\")\n      },\n      outputSchema: ForgePilotCaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema,\n      annotations: {\n        readOnlyHint: false,\n        destructiveHint: false,\n        idempotentHint: false,\n        openWorldHint: false\n      }\n    },\n    async ({ packetId, workerId, approval }) =>\n      runLoggedTool(\"forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry\", async () => {\n        const result = await captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry({ packetId, workerId, approval });\n        return structuredJsonResult(result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);\n      })\n  );\n\n  mcpServer.registerTool(\n    \"forgepilot_read_opencode_worker_telemetry\",\n    {\n      description: \"Read an existing OpenCode worker telemetry.json artifact without recapturing or mutating telemetry.\",\n      inputSchema: {\n        packetId: z.string().describe(\"Known ForgePilot packet id.\"),\n        workerId: z.string().describe(\"Existing OpenCode worker id.\")\n      },\n      outputSchema: ForgePilotReadOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema,\n      annotations: {\n        readOnlyHint: true,\n        destructiveHint: false,\n        idempotentHint: true,\n        openWorldHint: false\n      }\n    },\n    async ({ packetId, workerId }) =>\n      runLoggedTool(\"forgepilot_read_opencode_worker_telemetry\", async () => {\n        const result = await readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(packetId, workerId);\n        return structuredJsonResult(result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);\n      })\n  );\n\n'''\nif 'forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('  mcpServer.registerTool(\\n    \"forgepilot_start_opencode_worker\",', tools + '  mcpServer.registerTool(\\n    \"forgepilot_start_opencode_worker\",')\n\np.write_text(s)\nPY\nbash -ic 'pnpm build'\ngit status --short",

\"node:path\";\nimport { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, type WorkerStatus } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\n\nconst FORGEPILOT_REPO = \"/home/ridasaidd/forgepilot\";\nconst RUNS_ROOT = path.join(FORGEPILOT_REPO, \"runs\");\nconst SCHEMA_VERSION = \"forgepilot.opencode-worker-telemetry.v1\";\n\nexport interface CaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryInput {\n  packetId: string;\n  workerId: string;\n  approval: string;\n}\n\nexport type TerminalStateClassification =\n  | \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\"\n  | \"TERMINAL_FAILED\"\n  | \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\"\n  | \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\";\n\nexport interface ArtifactTelemetry {\n  path: string;\n  exists: boolean;\n  byteCount: number | null;\n  sha256: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry {\n  schemaVersion: string;\n  packetId: string;\n  workerId: string;\n  capturedAt: string;\n  workerStatus: WorkerStatus[\"status\"];\n  startedAt: string;\n  completedAt: string | null;\n  durationMs: number | null;\n  targetWorkspaceId: string;\n  workspacePath: string;\n  promptFile: string;\n  promptFileSha256: string;\n  modelId: string | null;\n  command: {\n    executable: string;\n    args: string[];\n    argsCount: number;\n  };\n  artifacts: {\n    status: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    startRequest: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    stdout: ArtifactTelemetry;\n    stderr: ArtifactTelemetry;\n  };\n  classifications: {\n    terminalState: TerminalStateClassification;\n    provenanceCompleteness: \"COMPLETE\" | \"INCOMPLETE\";\n    trustTier: \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\";\n    validationState: \"VALID\" | \"INCOMPLETE\";\n    admissionState: \"NOT_EVALUATED\";\n  };\n  telemetryPath: string;\n  observationSemantics: string;\n  reasons: string[];\n}\n\nfunction workerDir(packetId: string, workerId: string): string {\n  return path.join(RUNS_ROOT, packetId, \"opencode-workers\", workerId);\n}\n\nfunction telemetryPath(packetId: string, workerId: string): string {\n  return path.join(workerDir(packetId, workerId), \"telemetry.json\");\n}\n\nasync function artifactTelemetry(filePath: string): Promise<ArtifactTelemetry> {\n  try {\n    const [s, bytes] = await Promise.all([stat(filePath), readFile(filePath)]);\n    if (!s.isFile()) {\n      return { path: filePath, exists: false, byteCount: null, sha256: null };\n    }\n\n    return {\n      path: filePath,\n      exists: true,\n      byteCount: bytes.byteLength,\n      sha256: createHash(\"sha256\").update(bytes).digest(\"hex\"),\n    };\n  } catch {\n    return { path: filePath, exists: false, byteCount: null, sha256: null };\n  }\n}\n\nfunction durationMs(status: WorkerStatus): number | null {\n  if (status.completedAt === null) return null;\n  const started = Date.parse(status.startedAt);\n  const completed = Date.parse(status.completedAt);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(started) || !Number.isFinite(completed)) return null;\n  return Math.max(0, completed - started);\n}\n\nfunction classifyTerminalState(status: WorkerStatus[\"status\"]): TerminalStateClassification {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"SUCCEEDED\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\";\n    case \"FAILED\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_FAILED\";\n    case \"UNKNOWN_EXIT\":\n      return \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\";\n    case \"RUNNING\":\n      return \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction requiredFieldsPresent(status: WorkerStatus): boolean {\n  return Boolean(\n    status.workerId &&\n      status.packetId &&\n      status.targetWorkspaceId &&\n      status.workspacePath &&\n      status.promptFile &&\n      status.promptFileSha256 &&\n      status.startedAt &&\n      status.statusPath &&\n      status.startRequestPath &&\n      status.stdoutPath &&\n      status.stderrPath\n  );\n}\n\nfunction provenanceComplete(status: WorkerStatus, artifacts: OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry[\"artifacts\"]): boolean {\n  return (\n    requiredFieldsPresent(status) &&\n    artifacts.status.exists &&\n    artifacts.status.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.startRequest.exists &&\n    artifacts.startRequest.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.stdout.exists &&\n    artifacts.stdout.sha256 !== null &&\n    artifacts.stderr.exists &&\n    artifacts.stderr.sha256 !== null\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(input: CaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryInput): Promise<OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry> {\n  if (input.approval !== \"CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY\") {\n    throw new Error(`Approval must be \"CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY\", got \"${input.approval}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const status = await getOpenCodeWorkerStatus(input.packetId, input.workerId);\n  const telemetryFile = telemetryPath(input.packetId, input.workerId);\n\n  const artifacts = {\n    status: await artifactTelemetry(status.statusPath),\n    startRequest: await artifactTelemetry(status.startRequestPath),\n    stdout: await artifactTelemetry(status.stdoutPath),\n    stderr: await artifactTelemetry(status.stderrPath),\n  };\n\n  const complete = provenanceComplete(status, artifacts);\n  const reasons: string[] = [];\n  if (!complete) reasons.push(\"PROVENANCE_INCOMPLETE\");\n  if (status.status === \"RUNNING\") reasons.push(\"WORKER_STILL_RUNNING\");\n\n  const telemetry: OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry = {\n    schemaVersion: SCHEMA_VERSION,\n    packetId: input.packetId,\n    workerId: input.workerId,\n    capturedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    workerStatus: status.status,\n    startedAt: status.startedAt,\n    completedAt: status.completedAt,\n    durationMs: durationMs(status),\n    targetWorkspaceId: status.targetWorkspaceId,\n    workspacePath: status.workspacePath,\n    promptFile: status.promptFile,\n    promptFileSha256: status.promptFileSha256,\n    modelId: status.modelId,\n    command: {\n      executable: status.command.executable,\n      args: status.command.args,\n      argsCount: status.command.args.length,\n    },\n    artifacts,\n    classifications: {\n      terminalState: classifyTerminalState(status.status),\n      provenanceCompleteness: complete ? \"COMPLETE\" : \"INCOMPLETE\",\n      trustTier: \"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\",\n      validationState: complete ? \"VALID\" : \"INCOMPLETE\",\n      admissionState: \"NOT_EVALUATED\",\n    },\n    telemetryPath: telemetryFile,\n    observationSemantics: \"OpenCode worker telemetry is an observation, not admitted evidence or routing input by itself.\",\n    reasons,\n  };\n\n  await writeFile(telemetryFile, JSON.stringify(telemetry, null, 2) + \"\\n\", \"utf8\");\n  return telemetry;\n}\n\nexport async function readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(packetId: string, workerId: string): Promise<OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry> {\n  const filePath = telemetryPath(packetId, workerId);\n  try {\n    return JSON.parse(await readFile(filePath, \"utf8\")) as OpenCodeWorkerTelemetry;\n  } catch {\n    throw new Error(`Telemetry artifact does not exist: ${filePath}`);\n  }\n}\nEOF\n\npython3 - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\np = Path('src/server.ts')\ns = p.read_text()\nif 'opencode-worker-telemetry.js' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('import { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, readOpenCodeWorkerResult, startOpenCodeWorker } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\\n', 'import { getOpenCodeWorkerStatus, readOpenCodeWorkerResult, startOpenCodeWorker } from \"./opencode-worker.js\";\\nimport { captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry, readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry } from \"./opencode-worker-telemetry.js\";\\n')\n\nschema = '''\nconst ArtifactTelemetrySchema = z.object({\n  path: z.string(),\n  exists: z.boolean(),\n  byteCount: z.number().nullable(),\n  sha256: z.string().nullable()\n});\n\nconst OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema = {\n  schemaVersion: z.string(),\n  packetId: z.string(),\n  workerId: z.string(),\n  capturedAt: z.string(),\n  workerStatus: z.enum([\"RUNNING\", \"SUCCEEDED\", \"FAILED\", \"UNKNOWN_EXIT\"]),\n  startedAt: z.string(),\n  completedAt: z.string().nullable(),\n  durationMs: z.number().nullable(),\n  targetWorkspaceId: z.string(),\n  workspacePath: z.string(),\n  promptFile: z.string(),\n  promptFileSha256: z.string(),\n  modelId: z.string().nullable(),\n  command: z.object({\n    executable: z.string(),\n    args: z.array(z.string()),\n    argsCount: z.number()\n  }),\n  artifacts: z.object({\n    status: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    startRequest: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    stdout: ArtifactTelemetrySchema,\n    stderr: ArtifactTelemetrySchema\n  }),\n  classifications: z.object({\n    terminalState: z.enum([\"TERMINAL_SUCCEEDED\", \"TERMINAL_FAILED\", \"TERMINAL_UNKNOWN_EXIT\", \"NON_TERMINAL_RUNNING\"]),\n    provenanceCompleteness: z.enum([\"COMPLETE\", \"INCOMPLETE\"]),\n    trustTier: z.literal(\"TIER_2_VERIFIED_ARTIFACT\"),\n    validationState: z.enum([\"VALID\", \"INCOMPLETE\"]),\n    admissionState: z.literal(\"NOT_EVALUATED\")\n  }),\n  telemetryPath: z.string(),\n  observationSemantics: z.string(),\n  reasons: z.array(z.string())\n};\n\nconst ForgePilotCaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema = OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema;\nconst ForgePilotReadOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema = OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema;\n'''\nif 'OpenCodeWorkerTelemetrySchema' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('const OpenCodeWorkerStatusSchema = {\\n', schema + '\\nconst OpenCodeWorkerStatusSchema = {\\n')\n\ntools = '''\n  mcpServer.registerTool(\n    \"forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry\",\n    {\n      description: \"Capture normalized, non-admitted telemetry for an existing OpenCode worker and write telemetry.json under the worker directory.\",\n      inputSchema: {\n        packetId: z.string().describe(\"Known ForgePilot packet id.\"),\n        workerId: z.string().describe(\"Existing OpenCode worker id.\"),\n        approval: z.string().describe(\"Must exactly equal CAPTURE_OPENCODE_WORKER_TELEMETRY.\")\n      },\n      outputSchema: ForgePilotCaptureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema,\n      annotations: {\n        readOnlyHint: false,\n        destructiveHint: false,\n        idempotentHint: false,\n        openWorldHint: false\n      }\n    },\n    async ({ packetId, workerId, approval }) =>\n      runLoggedTool(\"forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry\", async () => {\n        const result = await captureOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry({ packetId, workerId, approval });\n        return structuredJsonResult(result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);\n      })\n  );\n\n  mcpServer.registerTool(\n    \"forgepilot_read_opencode_worker_telemetry\",\n    {\n      description: \"Read an existing OpenCode worker telemetry.json artifact without recapturing or mutating telemetry.\",\n      inputSchema: {\n        packetId: z.string().describe(\"Known ForgePilot packet id.\"),\n        workerId: z.string().describe(\"Existing OpenCode worker id.\")\n      },\n      outputSchema: ForgePilotReadOpenCodeWorkerTelemetryOutputSchema,\n      annotations: {\n        readOnlyHint: true,\n        destructiveHint: false,\n        idempotentHint: true,\n        openWorldHint: false\n      }\n    },\n    async ({ packetId, workerId }) =>\n      runLoggedTool(\"forgepilot_read_opencode_worker_telemetry\", async () => {\n        const result = await readOpenCodeWorkerTelemetry(packetId, workerId);\n        return structuredJsonResult(result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);\n      })\n  );\n\n'''\nif 'forgepilot_capture_opencode_worker_telemetry' not in s:\n    s = s.replace('  mcpServer.registerTool(\\n    \"forgepilot_start_opencode_worker\",', tools + '  mcpServer.registerTool(\\n    \"forgepilot_start_opencode_worker\",')\n\np.write_text(s)\nPY\nbash -ic 'pnpm build'\ngit status --short",

runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111049522Z-0783b01d-result.json:8:  "command": "mkdir -p runs/FP-META-034\n{\n  printf '%s\\n' '# FP-META-034 admission source search'\n  printf '%s\\n' ''\n  printf '%s\\n' '## Git baseline'\n  git branch --show-current\n  git rev-parse --short HEAD\n  git status --short\n  printf '%s\\n' ''\n  printf '%s\\n' '## Search: admission distribution/reporting sources'\n  grep -RInE \"admission_state|evidence_records|evidence_admission_events|admission_invalidation_events|NOT_EVALUATED|ADMITTED|PENDING|REJECTED|QUARANTINED|final-v1-project-report|project-report\" src docs packets runs package.json 2>/dev/null || true\n} > runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt\nnode - <<'NODE'\nconst fs = require('fs');\nconst outPath = 'runs/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json';\nconst result = {\n  packet_id: 'FP-META-034',\n  generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n  database_path: 'data/forgepilot.db',\n  method: 'readonly better-sqlite3 queries against current data/forgepilot.db',\n  query_status: 'NOT_RUN',\n  tables: {},\n  distributions: {},\n  notes: []\n};\ntry {\n  const Database = require('better-sqlite3');\n  if (!fs.existsSync('data/forgepilot.db')) {\n    result.query_status = 'DATABASE_NOT_FOUND';\n    fs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\n    process.exit(0);\n  }\n  const db = new Database('data/forgepilot.db', { readonly: true });\n  const tables = db.prepare(\"SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name\").all().map(r => r.name);\n  for (const name of ['evidence_records','evidence_admission_events','admission_invalidation_events','model_comparison_events']) {\n    result.tables[name] = tables.includes(name) ? 'PRESENT' : 'ABSENT';\n  }\n  function groupCount(table, col) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT ${col} AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table} GROUP BY ${col} ORDER BY ${col}`).all();\n  }\n  function count(table) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table}`).get().count;\n  }\n  result.query_status = 'OK';\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_total = count('evidence_records');\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_by_admission_state = groupCount('evidence_records', 'admission_state');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_total = count('evidence_admission_events');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_by_decision = tables.includes('evidence_admission_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT admission_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM evidence_admission_events GROUP BY admission_decision ORDER BY admission_decision').all() : null;\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_total = count('admission_invalidation_events');\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_by_decision = tables.includes('admission_invalidation_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT invalidation_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM admission_invalidation_events GROUP BY invalidation_decision ORDER BY invalidation_decision').all() : null;\n  if (tables.includes('evidence_records')) {\n    result.samples = db.prepare('SELECT evidence_id, packet_id, run_id, model_id, model_role, admission_state, validation_state, trust_tier, created_at FROM evidence_records ORDER BY evidence_id LIMIT 50').all();\n  }\n  result.notes.push('Counts are SQLite-derived from current data/forgepilot.db only.');\n  result.notes.push('Model trust labels are intentionally not counted as admission states.');\n  result.notes.push('NOT_EVALUATED is not admitted evidence.');\n  db.close();\n} catch (err) {\n  result.query_status = 'QUERY_FAILED';\n  result.error = String(err && err.stack ? err.stack : err);\n}\nfs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\nNODE\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- query json ---'\ncat runs/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json",

'PRESENT' : 'ABSENT';\n  }\n  function groupCount(table, col) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT ${col} AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table} GROUP BY ${col} ORDER BY ${col}`).all();\n  }\n  function count(table) {\n    if (!tables.includes(table)) return null;\n    return db.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM ${table}`).get().count;\n  }\n  result.query_status = 'OK';\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_total = count('evidence_records');\n  result.distributions.evidence_records_by_admission_state = groupCount('evidence_records', 'admission_state');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_total = count('evidence_admission_events');\n  result.distributions.evidence_admission_events_by_decision = tables.includes('evidence_admission_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT admission_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM evidence_admission_events GROUP BY admission_decision ORDER BY admission_decision').all() : null;\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_total = count('admission_invalidation_events');\n  result.distributions.admission_invalidation_events_by_decision = tables.includes('admission_invalidation_events') ? db.prepare('SELECT invalidation_decision AS state, COUNT(*) AS count FROM admission_invalidation_events GROUP BY invalidation_decision ORDER BY invalidation_decision').all() : null;\n  if (tables.includes('evidence_records')) {\n    result.samples = db.prepare('SELECT evidence_id, packet_id, run_id, model_id, model_role, admission_state, validation_state, trust_tier, created_at FROM evidence_records ORDER BY evidence_id LIMIT 50').all();\n  }\n  result.notes.push('Counts are SQLite-derived from current data/forgepilot.db only.');\n  result.notes.push('Model trust labels are intentionally not counted as admission states.');\n  result.notes.push('NOT_EVALUATED is not admitted evidence.');\n  db.close();\n} catch (err) {\n  result.query_status = 'QUERY_FAILED';\n  result.error = String(err && err.stack ? err.stack : err);\n}\nfs.writeFileSync(outPath, JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\nNODE\nprintf '%s\\n' '--- query json ---'\ncat runs/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json",

runs/FP-META-034/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T111032362Z-de0f3b6b-result.json:8:  "command": "cat > packets/FP-META-034.md <<'EOF'\n# FP-META-034 — V1 Admission Distribution Report\n\n## Task\n\nProduce a V1 admission-state distribution report from current ForgePilot evidence/admission storage.\n\n## Goal\n\nClarify how much of ForgePilot V1 evidence is actually admitted, pending, rejected, quarantined, or not evaluated.\n\nFP-META-034 answers one question:\n\n> What is the current admission-state distribution for V1 evidence records?\n\n## Background\n\nThe V1 completion report records capability completion, but completion is not admission. A V1 report that says capabilities are complete does not reveal whether supporting evidence is admitted.\n\nFP-META-030 clarified evidence-root boundaries. FP-META-031 clarified invalidation propagation semantics. FP-META-032 clarified that model trust labels are decoupled from admission distribution reporting.\n\n## Scope\n\nAdd:\n\n```text\nruns/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-query.json\nruns/FP-META-034/admission-distribution-report.md\nruns/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt\n```\n\nUpdate:\n\n```text\ndocs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md\n```\n\n## Required Method\n\nThe report must query the actual current store when possible instead of relying on narrative memory.\n\nIt must distinguish:\n\n- SQLite-derived counts,\n- file/report-derived observations,\n- implementation limitations,\n- absent tables or failed queries.\n\n## Required Boundary\n\nAdmission distribution must count evidence/admission states, not model-trust labels.\n\nThe report must not treat `NOT_EVALUATED` as admitted evidence.\n\n## Safety Requirements\n\nThis packet must not mutate the database, change admission logic, change reporting code, modify MCP bridge code, execute models, rank models, or claim admission where only completion exists.\n\n## Non-Goals\n\nThis packet does not implement a new reporting command, fix propagation, implement external workspaces, or admit evidence.\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n1. Packet exists.\n2. Admission distribution query artifact exists.\n3. Admission distribution report exists.\n4. Source/search evidence exists.\n5. Report clearly separates counts from interpretation.\n6. Report does not use model-trust labels as admission evidence.\n7. No implementation code changes are made.\n8. Final git status is clean after evidence is recorded.\nEOF\n\ngit add packets/FP-META-034.md\ngit commit -m \"Add FP-META-034 admission distribution packet\"\ngit log --oneline -5\ngit status --short",

runs/FP-META-034/admission-source-search.txt:521:runs/FP-MCP-158/terminal-commands/TERM-20260702T082157887Z-3130ce56-result.json:8:  "command": "set -e\ncat > docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md <<'EOF'\n# ForgePilot Progress Checklist\n\n## Purpose\n\nThis checklist is an evidence ledger index for ForgePilot V1 progress.\n\nIt answers:\n\n```text\nWhat exists, what proves it, what remains incomplete, and what must not be inferred yet?\n```\n\nThis checklist is not:\n\n- a roadmap,\n- a dashboard,\n- an implementation backlog,\n- a routing plan,\n- a model scorecard,\n- a replacement for packet evidence,\n- a machine-readable source of truth.\n\nThe source of truth remains committed packet artifacts, run artifacts, migrations, tests, and supported read-only query tools.\n\n## Update Rule\n\nNever mark something complete because we believe it works.\n\nMark it complete only because committed packet evidence proves it.\n\nRecommended update rhythm:\n\n```text\npacket implementation/evidence commit\n→ verification evidence recorded\n→ checklist updated only after the evidence boundary is crossed\n```\n\n## Status Vocabulary\n\n| Status | Meaning |\n|---|---|\n| `NOT_STARTED` | No packet or committed implementation evidence exists yet. |\n| `PACKET_RECORDED` | A packet exists, but implementation or verification evidence is not complete. |\n| `IMPLEMENTED` | Implementation exists in committed code, but verification or direct proof is incomplete. |\n| `VERIFIED` | Verification passed and is recorded, but a required direct MCP or operational proof is not complete. |\n| `DIRECT_MCP_PROVEN` | The capability was proven through the MCP tool surface where applicable. |\n| `RECORDED` | Evidence was recorded in committed run artifacts. |\n| `COMPLETE` | Packet exists, implementation/evidence is committed, verification passed, required direct proof passed, and repos were clean after recording. |\n| `PARTIAL` | Some evidence exists, but one or more required V1 criteria remain incomplete. |\n| `BLOCKED` | Progress is blocked by an external/manual/system condition. |\n| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed and not treated as a failure. |\n\n## ForgePilot V1 Completion Definition\n\nForgePilot V1 is complete when packet intent, execution, telemetry, verification, audit, admission, comparison, and reporting can be reconstructed from committed evidence and supported read-only queries, without relying on private chat memory.\n\nV1 is not complete merely because individual components exist.\n\nV1 is complete when a new operator or agent can inspect the repository and supported query surfaces and reconstruct what happened without relying on a previous ChatGPT session.\n\n## Current Baseline\n\nLast updated by:\n\n- packet: `FP-MCP-158`\n- ForgePilot branch: `main`\n- ForgePilot baseline before checklist implementation: `2d79a9a`\n- bridge branch: `feature/oauth-auth0`\n- bridge baseline: `3a16d68`\n\nRecent closed telemetry/query chain:\n\n- `FP-MCP-153` bounded OpenCode worker invocation\n- `FP-MCP-154` normalized worker telemetry capture\n- `FP-MCP-156` worker telemetry persistence\n- `FP-MCP-157` persisted telemetry query visibility\n\n## V1 Capability Checklist\n\n| Capability | Status | Evidence Packet(s) | Evidence / Notes | Boundary |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Packet lifecycle | `PARTIAL` | earlier packets, FP-MCP series | Packets are committed and used as work units. Full V1 lifecycle summary still needs a supported reporting view. | Packet existence is not execution evidence. |\n| Worker lifecycle | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded OpenCode worker invocation has direct MCP proof and committed worker lifecycle artifacts. | Worker lifecycle proof does not imply routing or autonomous execution. |\n| Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-154` | Normalized non-admitted worker telemetry capture exists and is directly readable. | Telemetry is observation, not admitted evidence. |\n| Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-156` | SQLite persistence exists, direct persist/read proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md`. | Persistence does not admit telemetry or make it routing input. |\n| Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Query tool implemented, verified, direct MCP query proof recorded in `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md`. | Querying makes telemetry visible, not meaningful. |\n| Verification evidence | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP evidence chain, earlier FP packets | Typecheck/build/test evidence exists for recent packets, but V1-wide verification reporting is not yet consolidated. | Passing verification is not admission. |\n| Audit evidence | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark/evaluation packets | Audit workflow exists in earlier ForgePilot work, but current FP-MCP chain does not yet expose a consolidated audit query/report view. | Audit is not comparison or admission by itself. |\n| Admission state | `PARTIAL` | FP-009, FP-010, FP-MCP-156/157 | Admission-state concepts and persistence exist; worker telemetry remains `NOT_EVALUATED`. V1 still needs a consolidated admission/reporting path. | `NOT_EVALUATED` must not be treated as admitted evidence. |\n| Comparison result | `PARTIAL` | earlier benchmark packets | Comparison artifacts exist in earlier evaluation workflows. V1 still needs a supported way to reconstruct comparison state from committed evidence/read-only queries. | Comparison must use admitted evidence only where evidence claims are made. |\n| Reporting / summary view | `NOT_STARTED` | none yet | No supported V1 report or summary view exists for packet/project state. | Manual raw-file inspection is not a V1 reporting surface. |\n| Progress checklist | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | This document creates the first repo-tracked V1 progress checklist. | Checklist is an index, not evidence itself. |\n| Completion criteria | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-158` | V1 completion definition is recorded in this document. | Completion criteria do not imply completion. |\n| Safety boundary documentation | `PARTIAL` | FP-MCP packets, boundary docs | Safety boundaries are repeatedly documented in packets and evidence. V1 still needs consolidated boundary reporting or index. | Safety statements must map back to enforced gates or evidence. |\n| Manual MCP Actions refresh process | `COMPLETE` | `FP-MCP-157` | Direct query proof records that new MCP tools require manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh before ChatGPT can discover them. | Bridge restart alone does not refresh ChatGPT action schema. |\n| SQLite checklist persistence | `DEFERRED` | `FP-MCP-158` | Markdown is intentionally used for v1 checklist. SQLite persistence may come later after vocabulary stabilizes. | Do not over-engineer checklist state before manual use proves shape. |\n| Routing | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | No model routing or optimization may be inferred. |\n| Model scoring/ranking | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Query results are not model-quality evidence. |\n| Dashboard UI | `DEFERRED` | none for V1 | Explicitly out of scope for current V1 completion work. | Reporting can be CLI/docs before dashboard UI. |\n\n## Recent Packet Evidence Index\n\n| Packet | Capability | Status | ForgePilot Commit(s) | Bridge Commit(s) | Direct MCP Proof | Evidence Artifact(s) | Caveat |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `FP-MCP-153` | Bounded worker invocation | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | worker lifecycle artifacts under `runs/FP-MCP-153/` | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-154` | Worker telemetry capture | `COMPLETE` | existing committed evidence | existing bridge support | PASS | telemetry artifacts under worker run directories | none recorded here |\n| `FP-MCP-156` | Worker telemetry persistence | `COMPLETE` | `e3702da`, `09c5094`, `d67c0cc`, `d1b03d5` | `5c2e7af`, `dc1a412` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-156/direct-mcp-smoke-result.md` | persisted telemetry remains non-admitted |\n| `FP-MCP-157` | Worker telemetry query visibility | `COMPLETE` | `dafa608`, `3b9d6e8`, `e91c91c`, `1e04c7f` | `3a16d68` | PASS | `runs/FP-MCP-157/direct-mcp-query-result.md` | manual OpenAI Actions schema refresh required for new MCP tools |\n| `FP-MCP-158` | Progress checklist v1 | `COMPLETE` | this packet | none | not applicable | `docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md` | documentation-only checklist |\n\n## Incomplete V1 Capability Notes\n\n### Reporting / Summary View\n\nStatus: `NOT_STARTED`\n\nRequired before V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect packet state without manually reading raw artifacts.\n- The reporting surface must distinguish observation, verification, audit, admission, and comparison.\n- Any evidence claims must clearly identify whether they are admitted or merely observed.\n\nNon-goals for initial reporting:\n\n- dashboard UI,\n- model ranking,\n- routing recommendations,\n- score calculation.\n\n### Verification Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Individual packets record typecheck/build/test/smoke evidence.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to summarize verification state across packets.\n- Clear distinction between verification pass and admission.\n\n### Audit Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Earlier ForgePilot benchmark/evaluation workflows include audit artifacts.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported way to inspect audit state across packets.\n- A clear index from packet to audit artifact to final outcome.\n\n### Admission / Comparison Reconstruction\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Admission-state vocabulary and persistence exist in earlier work.\n- Recent telemetry persistence records `admission_state` as `NOT_EVALUATED`.\n\nMissing for V1:\n\n- A supported read-only way to reconstruct admission/comparison state for a packet.\n- A reporting path that refuses to treat non-admitted observations as evidence.\n\n## Standing Operational Caveats\n\n### Manual OpenAI Actions Schema Refresh\n\nNew MCP tools are not discoverable by ChatGPT until the OpenAI Actions schema is manually refreshed from the Actions page.\n\nObserved in:\n\n- `FP-MCP-157`\n\nOperational implication:\n\n```text\nbridge implementation commit\n→ bridge build\n→ bridge service restart\n→ manual OpenAI Actions refresh\n→ direct ChatGPT MCP proof\n```\n\n### Telemetry Visibility Boundary\n\nTelemetry query visibility does not make telemetry meaningful.\n\nPersisted worker telemetry query results are observations, not admitted evidence or routing input.\n\n### Checklist Boundary\n\nThis checklist is not proof by itself.\n\nA checklist entry is trustworthy only to the extent that it points to committed packet evidence.\n\n## Deferred Ideas\n\nThese may be considered after V1 evidence/reporting surfaces stabilize:\n\n- SQLite-backed progress checklist persistence,\n- automated checklist generation,\n- dashboard UI,\n- telemetry aggregation,\n- cost/token analysis,\n- routing policy,\n- model scoring/ranking.\nEOF\n\ncat > runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt <<'EOF'\nFP-MCP-158 verification\n\nChecklist file exists:\nPASS\n- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md\n\nPacket file exists:\nPASS\n- packets/FP-MCP-158.md\n\nDocumentation-only scope:\nPASS\n- no implementation code files changed\n\nRequired content:\nPASS\n- status vocabulary present\n- V1 completion definition present\n- recent FP-MCP packet evidence index present\n- incomplete V1 capability entries present\n- manual OpenAI Actions refresh caveat present\n- checklist boundary recorded\n\nFinal cleanup:\nPASS pending final commit/status check\nEOF\n\ngit diff -- docs/forgepilot-progress-checklist.md runs/FP-MCP-158/verification.txt\ngit status --short",

routing recommendations,\n- score calculation.\n\n### Verification Evidence Consolidation\n\nStatus: `PARTIAL`\n\nWhat exists:\n\n- Individual packets record typecheck/build/test/smoke
